//! Independent security intent and conservative exposure obligations.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#3-sound-proof-boundary — a direct-rule theorem never clears an unmodeled path.
use super::{catalog::Catalog, Bound};
use crate::{ast, Diagnostic, Site};
use snapback4_core::schema::{RuleExpr, RuleTerm, Schema, Effect};
use serde::Serialize;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
pub mod kernel;
mod audit;
pub mod challenges;
mod lints;
mod operation;
mod concrete;
mod census;
mod observers;
mod temporal;
mod adoption;
mod images;
mod columns;
mod privilege;
mod authority_walk;
pub use audit::{Exposure, Grant};
pub use observers::Obligation;
pub use adoption::{draft, UncoveredExposure};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Invariant {
    pub table: String,
    pub target: String,
    pub column: Option<String>,
    pub predicate: String,
    pub site: Site,
    pub policy: Option<RuleExpr>,
    pub operation: Option<String>,
    pub requirements: Vec<RuleExpr>,
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub syntax: Option<ast::Expr>,
    pub unsupported: Option<String>,
}
impl Invariant {
    pub fn target_label(&self)->String {match &self.column{Some(c)=>format!("{}.{} {}",self.table,c,self.target),None=>format!("{}.{}",self.table,self.target)}}
}
pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Vec<Invariant> {
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for decl in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d| &d.invariants) {
        let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&decl.table) else {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET",format!("invariant names unknown table {}",decl.table),"declare the table or correct its name").guide("security")); continue;
        };
        let named_operation=operation::resolve(catalog,decl);
        if (!matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") && named_operation.is_none())
            || decl.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"&&!info.columns.contains_key(c)) {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET","invariant needs a declared column or table effect","use invariant table.read only <policy>, table.column write only <policy>, or table always <predicate>").guide("security")); continue;
        }
        let effect=match decl.target.as_str(){"insert"=>ast::Effect::Insert,"update"|"write"=>ast::Effect::Update,"delete"=>ast::Effect::Delete,_=>ast::Effect::Read};
        // Independent intent may use authorized reference operands even when a
        // runtime read gate must use captured authority. It never installs a gate.
        let effect=if effect==ast::Effect::Read{ast::Effect::Insert}else{effect};
        let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect,bindings:Vec::new(),value:decl.value.clone(),site:decl.site.clone()};
        let (policy,unsupported)=match super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule) {
            Ok(rule)=>(Some(rule),None),Err(error)=>match temporal::bind(catalog,info,&decl.value) {
                Some(rule) if decl.target=="read" => (Some(rule),None),
                _ => (None,Some(format!("policy outside rule proof fragment: {} ({})",error.message,error.code))),
            },
        };
        let requirements=named_operation.as_ref().map(|(_,p)| operation::requirements(catalog,info,p)).unwrap_or_default();
        out.push(Invariant{operation:named_operation.map(|(name,_)|name),requirements,syntax:Some(decl.value.clone()),table:decl.table.clone(),target:decl.target.clone(),column:decl.column.clone(),predicate:crate::print::expression(&decl.value),site:decl.site.clone(),policy,unsupported});
    }
    out
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all="lowercase")]
pub enum Status { Proved, Refuted, Unproved }
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Verdict {
    pub invariant: Invariant,
    pub status: Status,
    pub direct_rule: Status,
    pub paths: Vec<String>,
    pub reasons: Vec<String>,
    pub counterexample: Option<challenges::Counterexample>,
    pub obligations: Vec<Obligation>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Report {
    pub format: u32,
    pub scope: String,
    pub exclusions: Vec<String>,
    pub invariants: Vec<Verdict>,
    pub exposures: Vec<Exposure>,
    pub roles: BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>>,
    pub notes: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    pub uncovered: Vec<UncoveredExposure>,
    pub campaign_coverage: BTreeSet<String>,
}
impl Report {
    pub fn refuted(&self)->bool {self.invariants.iter().any(|v|v.status==Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability==challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace))}
    pub fn text(&self)->String {self.text_with_all(false)}
    pub fn text_with_all(&self,all:bool)->String {
        let mut out=format!("security: {}\n",self.scope);
        for v in &self.invariants {
            if !all&&v.status==Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability!=challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace){out.push_str(&format!("  {}: seed/synthetic refutation only (non-failing; --all for details)\n",v.invariant.target_label()));continue;}
            out.push_str(&format!("  {:?}: {}.{} {} — direct rule {:?}\n",v.status,v.invariant.table,format!("{}{}",v.invariant.column.as_ref().map(|c|format!("{c} ")).unwrap_or_default(),v.invariant.target),v.invariant.predicate,v.direct_rule));
            for reason in &v.reasons {out.push_str(&format!("    {reason}\n"));}
            for o in &v.obligations {out.push_str(&format!("    {:?}: {} — {}\n",o.status,o.path,o.reason));if let Some(repair)=&o.repair{out.push_str(&format!("      repair: {repair}\n"));}}
            if let Some(c)=&v.counterexample {out.push_str(&format!("    counterexample {}\n",serde_json::to_string(c).unwrap()));}
        }
        for note in &self.notes {out.push_str(&format!("  {} {}: {}\n    {}\n",note.site,note.code,note.message,note.rewrite));}
        for (role,entries) in &self.roles {
            out.push_str(&format!("role {role} (possible/conditional; row predicates below decide):\n"));
            for entry in entries {out.push_str(&format!("  {entry}\n"));}
        }
        for x in &self.exposures {
            out.push_str(&format!("table {} columns: {}\n",x.table,x.columns.iter().map(|(c,v)|format!("{c} ({v})")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")));
            for (column,audience) in &x.column_audiences {out.push_str(&format!("  column {column} audience: {audience}\n"));}
            for path in &x.paths {out.push_str(&format!("  {path}\n"));}
            for grant in &x.grants {out.push_str(&format!("  inherited grant {}: {}\n",grant.table,grant.predicate)); for w in &grant.writers {out.push_str(&format!("    writer {w}\n"));}}
        }
        for reason in &self.campaign_coverage{out.push_str(&format!("  campaign not covered: {reason}\n"));}
        out.push_str(&format!("  exclusions: {}\n",self.exclusions.join("; ")));
        out.push_str("exposures without an invariant (conditional role/row coverage; declarations are not proof):\n");
        for x in &self.uncovered {out.push_str(&format!("  {}.{}: {}; rows: {}\n",x.table,x.effect,x.audience,x.row_predicate));}
        if self.uncovered.is_empty(){out.push_str("  none\n");}out
    }
}
fn effect(word:&str)->Effect {match word {"read"=>Effect::Read,"insert"=>Effect::Insert,"update"=>Effect::Update,_=>Effect::Delete}}
fn effects(inv:&Invariant)->Vec<&str> { if inv.target=="write" {vec!["insert","update"]} else {vec![inv.target.as_str()]} }
/// Analyze declarations without using fixtures as proof assumptions.
pub fn analyze(bound:&Bound)->Report {
    let exposures=audit::exposures(bound);
    let mut verdicts=Vec::new();
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
        let mut reasons=Vec::new();let mut direct=Status::Proved;
        if let Some(why)=&inv.unsupported {reasons.push(why.clone());direct=Status::Unproved;}
        else if inv.target=="always" {
            // This narrow theorem holds on every validated committed image,
            // including seed/adoption, with no assumption about a writer.
            let nonnull=match inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap() {
                RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null))|
                RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null),RuleTerm::Field(f)) =>
                    table.columns.get(f).is_some_and(|t| !t.is_optional() && !matches!(t,snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Json{..}) && !table.engine_field(f)),
                _=>false,
            };
            if !nonnull {direct=Status::Unproved; reasons.push("row assertion is not implied by the implemented required-column theorem; transitions, counts and time require an additional proof".into());}
        } else if inv.operation.is_some() {
            match operation::prove(bound,inv) {Ok(true)=>{},Ok(false)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push("operation admission does not imply the policy; an actual operation challenge is required".into());},Err(e)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(e);}}
        } else {
            for word in effects(inv) {
                let admission=if word=="update" {table.update_rule_judged().into_owned()}else if word=="read" {columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())}else{table.rules.for_effect(effect(word)).clone()};
                let implication=if word=="update"{images::update(table,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())}else{kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())};
                match implication {
                    Ok(true)=>{},Ok(false)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(format!("{word} admission does not imply intent over independent predicates; a concrete replay is required for refutation"));},
                    Err(why)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(format!("{word}: {why}"));},
                }
                // Update implication judges both images. Only id/explicit
                // immutable local fields have the equal-image substitution;
                // owner-field and implicit writers remain separate blockers.
            }
        }
        reasons.extend(blockers(bound,inv));
        let obligations=observers::obligations(bound,inv);
        for o in &obligations {if o.status!=Status::Proved {reasons.push(format!("{}: {}; {}",o.path,o.reason,o.repair.as_deref().unwrap_or("declare an explicit observer policy")));}}
        let paths=exposures.iter().find(|x|x.table==inv.table).map(|x|x.paths.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
        let status=if direct==Status::Proved && reasons.is_empty(){Status::Proved}else{Status::Unproved};
        verdicts.push(Verdict{invariant:inv.clone(),status,direct_rule:direct,paths,reasons,counterexample:None,obligations});
    }
    let mut notes=lints::notes(bound,&exposures);
    let missing=adoption::missing(bound);
    if !missing.is_empty() {notes.push(Diagnostic::new(&Site::default(),"N_SECURITY_INTENT",format!("{} tables have access rules and no security invariants: {}; declare the spec's privacy rules as invariants (`guide security`)",missing.len(),missing.join(", ")),"review snapback/generated/security-invariants.q.draft against the spec; candidates are never auto-accepted").guide("security"));}
    let roles=audit::roles(&exposures);
    let uncovered=adoption::uncovered(bound,&exposures);
    Report{format:1,scope:"logical row/authority intent; all database snapshots; ordinary authenticated viewers including service seats; explicit unproved paths".into(),
        exclusions:vec!["system-principal trust boundary".into(),"physical timing, examined counts and resource/meter side channels".into(),"offline access may use a snapshot predating server revocation".into()],invariants:verdicts,exposures,roles,notes,uncovered,campaign_coverage:BTreeSet::new()}
}
fn blockers(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Vec<String> {
    let schema=&bound.schema;let t=&schema.tables[&inv.table];let mut out=BTreeSet::new();
    if inv.target=="read" && inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,policy)!=Ok(true)) {
        out.insert("wire sequencing/activity metadata has no logical-existence reduction; direct row-rule proof does not establish whole-protocol noninterference".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" {
        if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy {
            let grants=policy.disjuncts().into_iter().filter(|r|!r.any_term(&|term|!matches!(term,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_)))).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
            let global=RuleExpr::Or(grants);
            if !matches!(t.rules.insert,RuleExpr::Deny) && kernel::implies(&t.rules.insert,&global)!=Ok(true) {
                out.insert("insert/unique/upsert collision outcomes can reveal an existing hidden row; old-row read authorization is not proved by new-image insert authority".into());
            }
            if kernel::implies(&t.update_rule_judged(),&policy.old_and_next())!=Ok(true) && !matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny) {
                out.insert("blind update/upsert outcomes can reveal hidden-target existence; read correspondence is unproved".into());
            }
        }
        if !t.written_by.is_empty(){out.insert("owner-field write outcomes can observe targets outside the row read audience".into());}
    }
    if inv.target=="read" && t.columns.iter().any(|(c,k)| matches!(k.required(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Ref{..}) && t.on_delete.get(c).copied().unwrap_or(snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Restrict).acts()) {
        out.insert("parent deletion/referential refusal can observe this child (including default restrict and transitive closures)".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" && t.sync.is_some(){out.insert("device read uses a delivered snapshot; current server role at disconnected access is unproved".into());}
    if t.through.is_some(){out.insert("inherited/captured or live-parent authority requires a separate reduction (RULES5 owns live-parent semantics)".into());}
    if !schema.receipts.is_empty() || !schema.timelines.is_empty(){out.insert("receipt/timeline engine-owned paths require a separate reduction; no affected-table inference is assumed".into());}
    if t.is_expiring() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"delete"|"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("expiry/commit-end retirement can change rows outside ordinary delete rules".into());}
    if t.ephemeral.is_some() || !t.sequences.is_empty(){out.insert("ephemeral/sequence path is outside the current proof reduction".into());}
    if !t.written_by.is_empty() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("owned-field update bypass: table rule alone does not establish field-write authority".into());}
    for (name,other) in &schema.tables {
        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") && other.on_delete.values().any(|a|matches!(a,snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Cascade|snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::SetNull)) && name==&inv.table {
            out.insert("incoming cascade/set-null changes bypass this table's ordinary rules".into());
        }
        if inv.target=="read" {
            for rule in [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete] {
                if kernel::dependencies(rule).contains(&inv.table) {out.insert(format!("raw rule probe/reference from {name} can observe {} independently of its read rule",inv.table));}
            }
        }
    }
    if schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.target==inv.table) && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") {out.insert("aggregate/view target rows are written by engine maintenance independently of their direct table rules".into());}
    if !t.maintained.is_empty() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("engine-maintained columns bypass ordinary application write rules".into());}
    if bound.programs.iter().any(|p|matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action)|p.program.native)
        || !schema.effects.is_empty() || !schema.action_specs.is_empty() {
        out.insert("system/job/action/native/external path requires a reviewed reduction; no affected-table inference is assumed".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" {
        for p in &bound.programs {
            let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && !census::body(&p.program.body) {out.insert(format!("{}: scan/merge/loop or opaque IR path needs a reviewed read-disclosure reduction",p.program.name));}
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(target,_)|target!=&inv.table) {
                out.insert(format!("{} may copy protected data into another table; cross-table disclosure reduction is unproved",p.program.name));
            }
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && audit::authored_refusal(&json) {out.insert(format!("{}: authored refusal observations are not reduced to this read policy",p.program.name));}
        }
    }
    out.into_iter().collect()
}

pub fn add_source_notes(report:&mut Report,sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound){
    report.notes.extend(lints::source_notes(sources,bound));
    let mut sites=BTreeMap::new();
    for (file,source) in sources{let (doc,_)=crate::parse_document(file,source);for t in doc.tables{
        sites.insert(t.name.clone(),t.site.clone());for c in t.columns{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,c.name),c.site);}
        for r in t.rules{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,r.effect.word()),r.site);}
    }}
    for note in &mut report.notes{if note.site.line==0 {
        let key=note.message.split([' ',':']).next().unwrap_or("");
        note.site=sites.get(key).or_else(||sites.get(key.split('.').next().unwrap_or(""))).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Site::new(sources.first().map(|s|s.0.as_str()).unwrap_or("schema.q"),1,1));
    }}
}
use super::*;
impl Parser {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#2-surface — one existing expression grammar.
    pub(super) fn parse_invariant(&mut self) -> Fallible<()> {
        let site = self.take("invariant")?.site;
        let table = self.word()?.value;
        let (target, column) = if self.maybe(".") {
            let mut field = self.word()?.value;
            while self.maybe(".") {field.push('.');field.push_str(&self.word()?.value);}
            if self.maybe("write") { ("write".into(), Some(field)) }
            else if self.maybe("read") { ("read".into(), Some(field)) }
            else { (field, None) }
        } else { (self.take("always")?.value, None) };
        if target != "always" { self.take("only")?; }
        let value = self.expression(0, &[])?;
        self.end_line(&site)?;
        self.document.invariants.push(InvariantDecl { table, target, column, value, site });
        Ok(())
    }

}
//! Expand advisory write sites with lexical arguments and dominating guards.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#128-runtime-intent-and-independent-worlds — helper placement cannot hide a grant writer.
use super::*;
use ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramDecl,FunctionBody};
type Env=BTreeMap<String,Expr>;
fn resolve(expr:&Expr,env:&Env)->Expr{
    let mut expr=expr.clone();
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|{
        if let Expr::Object{fields,..}|Expr::Write{fields,..}=e{for f in fields{if f.value.is_none(){f.value=Some(env.get(&f.name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Expr::Name{name:f.name.clone(),site:f.site.clone()}));}}}
        if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e&&let Some(value)=env.get(name){*e=value.clone();}
    });expr
}
fn expression(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,guards:&[Stmt],depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    let mut expr=resolve(expr,env);
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|match e{
        Expr::Write{..}=>{let mut context=p.clone();context.prelude=Vec::new();context.body=guards.to_vec();out.push((e.clone(),context));},
        Expr::Call{name,args,..} if depth<32=>if let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(name){
            let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(arg,value)|(arg.name.clone(),value.clone())).collect();
            let mut inherited=guards.to_vec();
            match &f.body{
                FunctionBody::Expression(value)=>expression(catalog,p,value,&locals,&inherited,depth+1,out),
                FunctionBody::Block(body)=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut locals,&mut inherited,depth+1,out),
            }
        },_=>{}
    });
}
fn statements(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,body:&[Stmt],env:&mut Env,guards:&mut Vec<Stmt>,depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,env));}},
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
            guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()});
        },
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);break;},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out),
        Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
            statements(catalog,p,then_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);
            statements(catalog,p,else_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);
        },
        Stmt::For{domain,name,body,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,domain,env,guards,depth,out);let mut local=env.clone();local.remove(name);statements(catalog,p,body,&mut local,&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);},
        Stmt::As{body,..}=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out),_=>{}
    }}
}
pub(super) fn writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut env=Env::new();let mut guards=Vec::new();
    statements(catalog,p,&p.prelude,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);
    statements(catalog,p,&p.body,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);out
}
//! Finite three-valued implication, deliberately independent of database facts.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#3-sound-proof-boundary — impossible valuations overapproximate all snapshots.
use snapback4_core::schema::{RuleExpr as R, RuleTerm as T};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

pub const MAX_ATOMS: usize = 10;
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Truth { Yes, No, Unknown }
impl Truth {
    fn not(self) -> Self { match self { Self::Yes => Self::No, Self::No => Self::Yes, Self::Unknown => Self::Unknown } }
    fn combine(self, other: Self, and: bool) -> Self {
        let decisive = if and { Self::No } else { Self::Yes };
        if self == decisive || other == decisive { decisive }
        else if self == Self::Unknown || other == Self::Unknown { Self::Unknown }
        else { decisive.not() }
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
enum Formula { Constant(Truth), Atom(usize), Not(Box<Self>), And(Vec<Self>), Or(Vec<Self>) }
impl Formula {
    fn eval(&self, values: &[Truth]) -> Truth {
        match self {
            Self::Constant(v) => *v, Self::Atom(n) => values[*n], Self::Not(x) => x.eval(values).not(),
            Self::And(xs) => xs.iter().fold(Truth::Yes, |v, x| v.combine(x.eval(values), true)),
            Self::Or(xs) => xs.iter().fold(Truth::No, |v, x| v.combine(x.eval(values), false)),
        }
    }
}
fn term(t: &T) -> T { match t { T::NextField(f) => T::Field(f.clone()), other => other.clone() } }
fn atom(rule: &R) -> String {
    let mut r = rule.clone();
    match &mut r {
        R::Eq(a,b) | R::Compare(_,a,b) => { *a=term(a); *b=term(b); }
        R::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | R::Created { key, equalities, .. } => {
            for t in key { *t=term(t); }
            for (_,t) in equalities.iter_mut() { *t=term(t); }
            equalities.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    if let R::Eq(a,b)=&mut r && serde_json::to_string(a).unwrap()>serde_json::to_string(b).unwrap() { std::mem::swap(a,b); }
    let mut json=serde_json::to_value(&r).unwrap();
    // Provenance controls planning, not rule evaluation. Remove only this
    // leaf's metadata, never a similarly named literal-object field.
    for name in ["Exists","Created"] { if let Some(body)=json.get_mut(name).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object_mut) { body.remove("provenance"); } }
    json.to_string()
}
fn formula(rule: &R, atoms: &mut Vec<String>, nodes: &mut usize) -> Result<Formula, String> {
    *nodes+=1; if *nodes>1024 { return Err("formula exceeds 1024 nodes".into()); }
    Ok(match rule {
        R::Allow | R::Public {..} => Formula::Constant(Truth::Yes), R::Deny => Formula::Constant(Truth::No),
        R::And(xs) => Formula::And(xs.iter().map(|r| formula(r,atoms,nodes)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        R::Or(xs) => Formula::Or(xs.iter().map(|r| formula(r,atoms,nodes)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        R::Not(x) => Formula::Not(Box::new(formula(x,atoms,nodes)?)),
        R::Ne(a,b) => Formula::Not(Box::new(formula(&R::Eq(a.clone(),b.clone()),atoms,nodes)?)),
        R::Eq(T::Literal(a),T::Literal(b)) => Formula::Constant(if a==b {Truth::Yes}else{Truth::No}),
        R::Eq(T::Viewer,T::Viewer) => Formula::Constant(Truth::Yes),
        other => { let key=atom(other); let n=atoms.iter().position(|a| a==&key).unwrap_or_else(|| {atoms.push(key);atoms.len()-1});
            if atoms.len()>MAX_ATOMS {return Err(format!("more than {MAX_ATOMS} independent predicates"));} Formula::Atom(n) }
    })
}
/// `Ok(true)` is a proof; `Ok(false)` is only an abstract countermodel.
/// Neither limit exhaustion nor a countermodel is a concrete refutation.
pub fn implies(admission: &R, policy: &R) -> Result<bool,String> {
    let (mut atoms,mut nodes)=(Vec::new(),0);
    let a=formula(admission,&mut atoms,&mut nodes)?;
    let p=formula(policy,&mut atoms,&mut nodes)?;
    let mut values=vec![Truth::Unknown;atoms.len()];
    for mut n in 0..3usize.pow(atoms.len() as u32) {
        for v in &mut values { *v=match n%3 {0=>Truth::Yes,1=>Truth::No,_=>Truth::Unknown}; n/=3; }
        if a.eval(&values)==Truth::Yes && p.eval(&values)!=Truth::Yes { return Ok(false); }
    }
    Ok(true)
}
pub fn dependencies(rule: &R) -> BTreeSet<String> {
    let mut out=BTreeSet::new();
    match rule {
        R::Exists{table,key,equalities,..}|R::Created{table,key,equalities,..} => {out.insert(table.clone());for term in key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)){for (table,_) in term.references(){out.insert(table.into());}}}
        R::And(xs)|R::Or(xs) => for x in xs {out.extend(dependencies(x));}, R::Not(x) => out.extend(dependencies(x)),
        R::Eq(a,b)|R::Ne(a,b)|R::Compare(_,a,b) => for t in [a,b] { for (table,_) in t.references() {out.insert(table.into());} },
        _=>{}
    }
    out
}
#[cfg(test)] mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn a() -> R { R::Eq(T::Field("owner".into()),T::Viewer) }
    #[test] fn kleene_unknown_is_neither_true_nor_false() {
        assert_eq!(Truth::Unknown.not(),Truth::Unknown);
        assert_eq!(Truth::No.combine(Truth::Unknown,true),Truth::No);
        assert_eq!(Truth::Yes.combine(Truth::Unknown,false),Truth::Yes);
        assert!(!implies(&R::Allow,&R::Or(vec![a(),R::Not(Box::new(a()))])).unwrap());
    }
    #[test] fn no_boolean_assignment_is_a_concrete_refutation() {
        assert!(implies(&R::And(vec![a(),R::Deny]),&R::Deny).unwrap());
        assert!(!implies(&R::Allow,&a()).unwrap());
        assert!(implies(&a(),&R::Eq(T::Viewer,T::Field("owner".into()))).unwrap());
        let xs=(0..11).map(|n|R::Eq(T::Field(format!("f{n}")),T::Viewer)).collect();
        assert!(implies(&R::And(xs),&a()).is_err());
    }
}
//! Partial typed row-policy interpreter, never used to establish a proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown/unsupported is not false.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Literal};
use snapback4_core::Value;
type Rows=BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>;
fn boolean(v:Option<Value>)->Option<bool>{match v{Some(Value::Bool(v))=>Some(v),_=>None}}
pub(super) fn evaluate(expr:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<bool>{boolean(value(expr,row,rows,schema,viewer))}
fn value(e:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<Value>{Some(match e{
    Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"=>Value::Id(viewer.into()),
    Expr::Literal{value,..}=>match value{Literal::Str(s)=>Value::String(s.clone()),Literal::Bool(v)=>Value::Bool(*v),Literal::Null=>Value::Null,Literal::Number(s)=>Value::Int(s.parse().ok()?)},
    Expr::Focal{..}=>row.clone(),
    Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..})=>row.field(name).ok()?.clone(),
    Expr::Unary{op,value:x,..} if op=="not"=>Value::Bool(!boolean(value(x,row,rows,schema,viewer))?),
    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..} if op=="and"||op=="or"=>{
        let a=boolean(value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer));let b=boolean(value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer));
        let and=op=="and";let decisive=!and;
        Value::Bool(if a==Some(decisive)||b==Some(decisive){decisive}else if a.is_some()&&b.is_some(){and}else{return None;})
    },
    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=>{
        let a=value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;let b=value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;
        if matches!(op.as_str(),"+"|"-"|"*") {
            let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b))=(a,b)else{return None;};
            let n=match op.as_str(){"+"=>i128::from(a)+i128::from(b),"-"=>i128::from(a)-i128::from(b),_=>i128::from(a)*i128::from(b)};
            return Value::int(n).ok();
        }
        // The binder contextually types principal/id literals. This partial
        // evaluator refuses that coercion rather than using host strings.
        if !matches!((&a,&b),(Value::Id(_),Value::Id(_))|(Value::String(_),Value::String(_))|(Value::Int(_),Value::Int(_))|(Value::Bool(_),Value::Bool(_))|(Value::Null,_)|(_,Value::Null)){return None;}
        Value::Bool(match op.as_str(){"="=>a==b,"!="=>a!=b,"<"|"<="|">"|">="=>if let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b))=(a,b){match op.as_str(){"<"=>a<b,"<="=>a<=b,">"=>a>b,_=>a>=b}}else{return None;},_=>return None})
    },
    Expr::Call{name,args,..} if (name=="count"||name=="len")&&args.len()==1 => {
        let Value::Array(values)=value(&args[0],row,rows,schema,viewer)?else{return None;};
        Value::Int(values.len().try_into().ok()?)
    },
    Expr::Call{name,args,..} if name=="sum"&&args.len()==2 => {
        let Value::Array(values)=value(&args[0],row,rows,schema,viewer)?else{return None;};
        let sum=values.iter().try_fold(0i128,|sum,r|match value(&args[1],r,rows,schema,viewer)? {Value::Int(n)=>sum.checked_add(i128::from(n)),_=>None})?;
        Value::int(sum).ok()?
    },
    Expr::Array{items,..}=>Value::Array(items.iter().map(|e|value(e,row,rows,schema,viewer)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
    Expr::Read(read)|Expr::Exists{read,..}=>{
        let t=schema.table(&read.table)?;
        if t.is_expiring()||t.not_before.is_some()||t.through.is_some()||schema.maintain_for(&read.table).is_some()||read.filter.is_some()||read.range.is_some()||!read.seeks.is_empty()||read.matching.is_some(){return None;}
        let mut pins=Vec::new();
        for key in &read.keys{let column=key.column.as_ref()?;let val=value(&key.value,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;
            let kind=t.columns.get(column)?;
            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
            let typed=match (kind.required(),&val) {
                (_,Value::Null)=>kind.is_optional(),
                (C::Id|C::Principal|C::Ref{..},Value::Id(_))=>true,
                (C::Text{..},Value::String(_))=>true,
                (C::Enum{variants},Value::String(v))=>variants.contains(v),
                (C::Int|C::IntRange{..}|C::Time,Value::Int(_))=>true,
                (C::Bool,Value::Bool(_))=>true,_=>false,
            };
            if !typed{return None;}pins.push((column,val));}
        // Integrity assertions quantify over the complete raw snapshot, not
        // a caller-filtered program population. Temporal/captured populations
        // above remain unsupported. No solver proof uses these samples.
        let selected=rows.get(&read.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|pins.iter().all(|(c,v)|r.field(c).ok()==Some(v))).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if matches!(e,Expr::Exists{..}) {Value::Bool(!selected.is_empty())}else{Value::Array(selected)}
    },
    _=>return None,
})}

//! Expand advisory write sites with lexical arguments and dominating guards.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#128-runtime-intent-and-independent-worlds — helper placement cannot hide a grant writer.
use super::*;
use ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramDecl,FunctionBody};
type Env=BTreeMap<String,Expr>;
fn resolve(expr:&Expr,env:&Env)->Expr{
    let mut expr=expr.clone();
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|{
        if let Expr::Object{fields,..}|Expr::Write{fields,..}=e{for f in fields{if f.value.is_none(){f.value=Some(env.get(&f.name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Expr::Name{name:f.name.clone(),site:f.site.clone()}));}}}
        if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e&&let Some(value)=env.get(name){*e=value.clone();}
    });expr
}
fn expression(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,guards:&[Stmt],depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    let mut expr=resolve(expr,env);
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|match e{
        Expr::Write{..}=>{let mut context=p.clone();context.prelude=Vec::new();context.body=guards.to_vec();out.push((e.clone(),context));},
        Expr::Call{name,args,..} if depth<32=>if let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(name){
            let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(arg,value)|(arg.name.clone(),value.clone())).collect();
            let mut inherited=guards.to_vec();
            match &f.body{
                FunctionBody::Expression(value)=>expression(catalog,p,value,&locals,&inherited,depth+1,out),
                FunctionBody::Block(body)=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut locals,&mut inherited,depth+1,out),
            }
        },_=>{}
    });
}
fn statements(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,body:&[Stmt],env:&mut Env,guards:&mut Vec<Stmt>,depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,env));}},
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
            guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()});
        },
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);break;},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out),
        Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
            statements(catalog,p,then_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);
            statements(catalog,p,else_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);
        },
        Stmt::For{domain,name,body,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,domain,env,guards,depth,out);let mut local=env.clone();local.remove(name);statements(catalog,p,body,&mut local,&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);},
        Stmt::As{body,..}=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out),_=>{}
    }}
}
pub(super) fn writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut env=Env::new();let mut guards=Vec::new();
    statements(catalog,p,&p.prelude,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);
    statements(catalog,p,&p.body,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);out
}
//! Finite three-valued implication, deliberately independent of database facts.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#3-sound-proof-boundary — impossible valuations overapproximate all snapshots.
use snapback4_core::schema::{RuleExpr as R, RuleTerm as T};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

pub const MAX_ATOMS: usize = 10;
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Truth { Yes, No, Unknown }
impl Truth {
    fn not(self) -> Self { match self { Self::Yes => Self::No, Self::No => Self::Yes, Self::Unknown => Self::Unknown } }
    fn combine(self, other: Self, and: bool) -> Self {
        let decisive = if and { Self::No } else { Self::Yes };
        if self == decisive || other == decisive { decisive }
        else if self == Self::Unknown || other == Self::Unknown { Self::Unknown }
        else { decisive.not() }
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
enum Formula { Constant(Truth), Atom(usize), Not(Box<Self>), And(Vec<Self>), Or(Vec<Self>) }
impl Formula {
    fn eval(&self, values: &[Truth]) -> Truth {
        match self {
            Self::Constant(v) => *v, Self::Atom(n) => values[*n], Self::Not(x) => x.eval(values).not(),
            Self::And(xs) => xs.iter().fold(Truth::Yes, |v, x| v.combine(x.eval(values), true)),
            Self::Or(xs) => xs.iter().fold(Truth::No, |v, x| v.combine(x.eval(values), false)),
        }
    }
}
fn term(t: &T) -> T { match t { T::NextField(f) => T::Field(f.clone()), other => other.clone() } }
fn atom(rule: &R) -> String {
    let mut r = rule.clone();
    match &mut r {
        R::Eq(a,b) | R::Compare(_,a,b) => { *a=term(a); *b=term(b); }
        R::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | R::Created { key, equalities, .. } => {
            for t in key { *t=term(t); }
            for (_,t) in equalities.iter_mut() { *t=term(t); }
            equalities.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    if let R::Eq(a,b)=&mut r && serde_json::to_string(a).unwrap()>serde_json::to_string(b).unwrap() { std::mem::swap(a,b); }
    let mut json=serde_json::to_value(&r).unwrap();
    // Provenance controls planning, not rule evaluation. Remove only this
    // leaf's metadata, never a similarly named literal-object field.
    for name in ["Exists","Created"] { if let Some(body)=json.get_mut(name).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object_mut) { body.remove("provenance"); } }
    json.to_string()
}
fn formula(rule: &R, atoms: &mut Vec<String>, nodes: &mut usize) -> Result<Formula, String> {
    *nodes+=1; if *nodes>1024 { return Err("formula exceeds 1024 nodes".into()); }
    Ok(match rule {
        R::Allow | R::Public {..} => Formula::Constant(Truth::Yes), R::Deny => Formula::Constant(Truth::No),
        R::And(xs) => Formula::And(xs.iter().map(|r| formula(r,atoms,nodes)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        R::Or(xs) => Formula::Or(xs.iter().map(|r| formula(r,atoms,nodes)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        R::Not(x) => Formula::Not(Box::new(formula(x,atoms,nodes)?)),
        R::Ne(a,b) => Formula::Not(Box::new(formula(&R::Eq(a.clone(),b.clone()),atoms,nodes)?)),
        R::Eq(T::Literal(a),T::Literal(b)) => Formula::Constant(if a==b {Truth::Yes}else{Truth::No}),
        R::Eq(T::Viewer,T::Viewer) => Formula::Constant(Truth::Yes),
        other => { let key=atom(other); let n=atoms.iter().position(|a| a==&key).unwrap_or_else(|| {atoms.push(key);atoms.len()-1});
            if atoms.len()>MAX_ATOMS {return Err(format!("more than {MAX_ATOMS} independent predicates"));} Formula::Atom(n) }
    })
}
/// `Ok(true)` is a proof; `Ok(false)` is only an abstract countermodel.
/// Neither limit exhaustion nor a countermodel is a concrete refutation.
pub fn implies(admission: &R, policy: &R) -> Result<bool,String> {
    let (mut atoms,mut nodes)=(Vec::new(),0);
    let a=formula(admission,&mut atoms,&mut nodes)?;
    let p=formula(policy,&mut atoms,&mut nodes)?;
    let mut values=vec![Truth::Unknown;atoms.len()];
    for mut n in 0..3usize.pow(atoms.len() as u32) {
        for v in &mut values { *v=match n%3 {0=>Truth::Yes,1=>Truth::No,_=>Truth::Unknown}; n/=3; }
        if a.eval(&values)==Truth::Yes && p.eval(&values)!=Truth::Yes { return Ok(false); }
    }
    Ok(true)
}
pub fn dependencies(rule: &R) -> BTreeSet<String> {
    let mut out=BTreeSet::new();
    match rule {
        R::Exists{table,key,equalities,..}|R::Created{table,key,equalities,..} => {out.insert(table.clone());for term in key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)){for (table,_) in term.references(){out.insert(table.into());}}}
        R::And(xs)|R::Or(xs) => for x in xs {out.extend(dependencies(x));}, R::Not(x) => out.extend(dependencies(x)),
        R::Eq(a,b)|R::Ne(a,b)|R::Compare(_,a,b) => for t in [a,b] { for (table,_) in t.references() {out.insert(table.into());} },
        _=>{}
    }
    out
}
#[cfg(test)] mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn a() -> R { R::Eq(T::Field("owner".into()),T::Viewer) }
    #[test] fn kleene_unknown_is_neither_true_nor_false() {
        assert_eq!(Truth::Unknown.not(),Truth::Unknown);
        assert_eq!(Truth::No.combine(Truth::Unknown,true),Truth::No);
        assert_eq!(Truth::Yes.combine(Truth::Unknown,false),Truth::Yes);
        assert!(!implies(&R::Allow,&R::Or(vec![a(),R::Not(Box::new(a()))])).unwrap());
    }
    #[test] fn no_boolean_assignment_is_a_concrete_refutation() {
        assert!(implies(&R::And(vec![a(),R::Deny]),&R::Deny).unwrap());
        assert!(!implies(&R::Allow,&a()).unwrap());
        assert!(implies(&a(),&R::Eq(T::Viewer,T::Field("owner".into()))).unwrap());
        let xs=(0..11).map(|n|R::Eq(T::Field(format!("f{n}")),T::Viewer)).collect();
        assert!(implies(&R::And(xs),&a()).is_err());
    }
}
//! Partial typed row-policy interpreter, never used to establish a proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown/unsupported is not false.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Literal};
use snapback4_core::Value;
type Rows=BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>;
fn boolean(v:Option<Value>)->Option<bool>{match v{Some(Value::Bool(v))=>Some(v),_=>None}}
pub(super) fn evaluate(expr:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<bool>{boolean(value(expr,row,rows,schema,viewer))}
fn value(e:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<Value>{Some(match e{
    Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"=>Value::Id(viewer.into()),
    Expr::Literal{value,..}=>match value{Literal::Str(s)=>Value::String(s.clone()),Literal::Bool(v)=>Value::Bool(*v),Literal::Null=>Value::Null,Literal::Number(s)=>Value::Int(s.parse().ok()?)},
    Expr::Focal{..}=>row.clone(),
    Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..})=>row.field(name).ok()?.clone(),
    Expr::Unary{op,value:x,..} if op=="not"=>Value::Bool(!boolean(value(x,row,rows,schema,viewer))?),
    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..} if op=="and"||op=="or"=>{
        let a=boolean(value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer));let b=boolean(value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer));
        let and=op=="and";let decisive=!and;
        Value::Bool(if a==Some(decisive)||b==Some(decisive){decisive}else if a.is_some()&&b.is_some(){and}else{return None;})
    },
    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=>{
        let a=value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;let b=value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;
        if matches!(op.as_str(),"+"|"-"|"*") {
            let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b))=(a,b)else{return None;};
            let n=match op.as_str(){"+"=>i128::from(a)+i128::from(b),"-"=>i128::from(a)-i128::from(b),_=>i128::from(a)*i128::from(b)};
            return Value::int(n).ok();
        }
        // The binder contextually types principal/id literals. This partial
        // evaluator refuses that coercion rather than using host strings.
        if !matches!((&a,&b),(Value::Id(_),Value::Id(_))|(Value::String(_),Value::String(_))|(Value::Int(_),Value::Int(_))|(Value::Bool(_),Value::Bool(_))|(Value::Null,_)|(_,Value::Null)){return None;}
        Value::Bool(match op.as_str(){"="=>a==b,"!="=>a!=b,"<"|"<="|">"|">="=>if let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b))=(a,b){match op.as_str(){"<"=>a<b,"<="=>a<=b,">"=>a>b,_=>a>=b}}else{return None;},_=>return None})
    },
    Expr::Call{name,args,..} if (name=="count"||name=="len")&&args.len()==1 => {
        let Value::Array(values)=value(&args[0],row,rows,schema,viewer)?else{return None;};
        Value::Int(values.len().try_into().ok()?)
    },
    Expr::Call{name,args,..} if name=="sum"&&args.len()==2 => {
        let Value::Array(values)=value(&args[0],row,rows,schema,viewer)?else{return None;};
        let sum=values.iter().try_fold(0i128,|sum,r|match value(&args[1],r,rows,schema,viewer)? {Value::Int(n)=>sum.checked_add(i128::from(n)),_=>None})?;
        Value::int(sum).ok()?
    },
    Expr::Array{items,..}=>Value::Array(items.iter().map(|e|value(e,row,rows,schema,viewer)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
    Expr::Read(read)|Expr::Exists{read,..}=>{
        let t=schema.table(&read.table)?;
        if t.is_expiring()||t.not_before.is_some()||t.through.is_some()||schema.maintain_for(&read.table).is_some()||read.filter.is_some()||read.range.is_some()||!read.seeks.is_empty()||read.matching.is_some(){return None;}
        let mut pins=Vec::new();
        for key in &read.keys{let column=key.column.as_ref()?;let val=value(&key.value,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;
            let kind=t.columns.get(column)?;
            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
            let typed=match (kind.required(),&val) {
                (_,Value::Null)=>kind.is_optional(),
                (C::Id|C::Principal|C::Ref{..},Value::Id(_))=>true,
                (C::Text{..},Value::String(_))=>true,
                (C::Enum{variants},Value::String(v))=>variants.contains(v),
                (C::Int|C::IntRange{..}|C::Time,Value::Int(_))=>true,
                (C::Bool,Value::Bool(_))=>true,_=>false,
            };
            if !typed{return None;}pins.push((column,val));}
        // Integrity assertions quantify over the complete raw snapshot, not
        // a caller-filtered program population. Temporal/captured populations
        // above remain unsupported. No solver proof uses these samples.
        let selected=rows.get(&read.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|pins.iter().all(|(c,v)|r.field(c).ok()==Some(v))).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if matches!(e,Expr::Exists{..}) {Value::Bool(!selected.is_empty())}else{Value::Array(selected)}
    },
    _=>return None,
})}
//! Conservative transitive write closure for pre-write policy snapshots.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#127-stable-judgments-and-direct-payload-witnesses — implicit writes cannot establish pre-write intent.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn implicit(bound:&Bound,p:&Program)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut affected=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut implicit=BTreeSet::new();
    loop{
        let before=affected.len();
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if affected.contains(&m.source)||m.row_key.as_ref().is_some_and(|r|affected.contains(&r.table)){
            if bound.schema.table(&m.target).is_some(){implicit.insert(m.target.clone());affected.insert(m.target.clone());}
            if let Some(owner)=&m.private{implicit.insert(owner.table.clone());affected.insert(owner.table.clone());}
        }}
        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{
            if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|edge.live.is_some()&&affected.contains(&edge.parent)){implicit.insert(name.clone());affected.insert(name.clone());}
            for (column,action) in &t.on_delete{
            if action.writes()&&let Some(C::Ref{table})=t.columns.get(column).map(C::required)&&affected.contains(table){implicit.insert(name.clone());affected.insert(name.clone());}
        }}
        if before==affected.len(){break;}
    }implicit
}

fn dependencies(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut deps=kernel::dependencies(rule);
    loop{let before=deps.len();for name in deps.clone(){if let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(&name){deps.extend(kernel::dependencies(&t.rules.read));if let Some(edge)=&t.through{deps.insert(edge.parent.clone());}}}if before==deps.len(){return deps;}}
}
/// A stable necessary condition may refute an intent even when another
/// conjunct changes. Dropping a requirement never proves the full policy.
pub(super) fn necessary(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,inv:&Invariant)->Option<Invariant>{
    let policy=inv.policy.as_ref()?;let implicit=implicit(bound,p);
    if implicit.contains(&inv.table){return None;}
    let mut changed=implicit;
    changed.extend(ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t!=&inv.table).map(|(t,_)|t));
    fn weak(bound:&Bound,r:&RuleExpr,changed:&BTreeSet<String>)->RuleExpr{
        match r{
            RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|weak(bound,r,changed)).collect()),
            RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|weak(bound,r,changed)).collect()),
            _ if dependencies(bound,r).iter().any(|d|changed.contains(d))=>RuleExpr::Allow,
            _=>r.clone(),
        }
    }
    let weaker=weak(bound,policy,&changed);
    if kernel::implies(policy,&weaker)!=Ok(true){return None;}
    let mut judged=inv.clone();judged.policy=Some(weaker);Some(judged)
}

   110	            }
   111	        }}
   112	    }
   113	}
   114	fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
   115	    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
   116	}
   117	fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   118	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
   119	        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
   120	            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
   121	            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
   122	            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
   123	            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
   124	            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
   125	                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
   126	            }
   127	        }
   128	    }
   129	}
   130	fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   131	    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
   132	    if !super::super::census::body(&p.body){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: loop/opaque write boundary requires executed-effect tracing",p.name));return;}
   133	    for inv in &bound.invariants{
   134	        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always")||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
   135	        let judged;
   136	        let inv=if inv.target=="always"{inv}else{
   137	            let Some(stable)=stability::necessary(bound,p,inv)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: unknown/implicit policy changes need executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;};
   138	            judged=stable;&judged
   139	        };
   140	        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
   141	        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   142	        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
   143	        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1!="delete"&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: commit-end/implicit retirement is not an executed delete",p.name));}
   144	        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1=="delete"{
   145	            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|live(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],old)&&!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
   146	                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
   147	                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
   148	                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
   149	            }
   150	        }
   151	        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
   152	            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()&&live(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],r));
   153	            if old==Some(row){continue;}
   154	            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
   155	            if inv.target!="always"&&inv.operation.is_none()&&!super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
   156	            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
   157	            if inv.target=="always" {
   158	                if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
   159	            }else{
   160	                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
   161	                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table) {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: write policy dependency changes within the operation; tracing required",p.name));continue;}
   162	                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row,prior)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false)){
   163	                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
   164	                }
   165	            }
   166	        }
   167	    }
   168	}
   169	fn role_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
   170	    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
   171	    let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
   172	    let Ok(mut judge)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return out;};
   173	    for (table,_) in ir::write_tables(p){
   174	        let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&table);
   175	        if !columns.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))){continue;}
   176	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
   177	        for row in initial.get(&table).into_iter().flatten(){
   178	            let principal=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal).then(||row.field(c).ok()).flatten());
   179	            let Some(principal)=principal else{continue;};
   180	            let mut targets=Vec::new();
   181	            for case in cases{
   182	                let mut args=case.clone();
   183	                for (name,kind) in &p.args{match kind{
   184	                    ir::ArgType::Principal=>{args.insert(name.clone(),principal.clone());},
   185	                    ir::ArgType::Ref{table:target} if target==&table=>{if let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(name.clone(),id.clone());}},_=>{}
   186	                }}
   187	                if !targets.contains(&args){targets.push(args);}
   188	            }
   189	            let mut row_cases=Vec::new();
   190	            for actor in actors{
   191	                let owner=columns.iter().filter_map(|c|t.written_by.get(c)).any(|a|row.field(&a.owner).ok()==Some(&Value::Id(actor.clone())));
   192	                let gate=judge.fixture_policy(&t.update_rule_judged(),actor,Some(row),row,NOW).ok();
   193	                if !owner&&gate!=Some(rules::Verdict::Readable){continue;}
   194	                for args in &targets{row_cases.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
   195	            }
   196	            groups.push(row_cases);
   197	        }
   198	    }
   199	    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(case)=g.get(n){out.push(case.clone());}}}
   200	    out
   201	}
   202	pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   203	    if bound.invariants.is_empty(){return;}
   204	    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   205	    if initial.values().all(Vec::is_empty){out.unsupported.insert("history needs a trusted fixture; generated snapshots have separate reachability labels".into());return;}
   206	    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
   207	    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
   208	    let probes=Vec::new();
   209	    let mut groups=Vec::new();
   210	    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
   211	        let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program.args,&initial,&actors,out);
   212	        let mut group=role_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture);
   213	        // Diagonal order gives both actors and argument values an early turn.
   214	        for shift in 0..actors.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(actors.len()){
   215	            if cases.is_empty(){break;}
   216	            group.push((&bp.program,actors[(n+shift)%actors.len()].clone(),cases[n%cases.len()].clone()));
   217	        }}
   218	        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
   219	    }
   220	    let mut candidates=Vec::new();
   221	    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){candidates.push(c.clone());}}}
   222	    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   223	    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
   224	    // Breadth first, then diagonal pairs: no first step monopolizes the cap.
   225	    let singles=candidates.len().min(LIMIT);
   226	    for n in 0..singles+LIMIT {
   227	        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let k=n-singles;vec![k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()]};
   228	        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   229	        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
   230	        for i in indices{
   231	            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   232	            let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history asset arguments: {why}",p.name));break;}};
   233	            let mut prior=if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){match fork(bound,&engine,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>Some(e),Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);None}}}else{None};
   234	            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   235	            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
   236	                Ok(result)=>{
   237	                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
   238	                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
   239	                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   240	                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
   241	                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
   242	                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
   243	            }
   244	        }
   245	    }
   246	    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
   247	}

codex
The code has dedicated paths for authorized reference-policy evaluation, cross-table assertion rechecks, and helper write expansion. I’ll check their dependency and snapshot handling for missed violations or false counterexamples.

    36	fn needed(schema:&Schema,name:&str,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){
    37	    if !out.insert(name.into()){return;}let Some(t)=schema.table(name)else{return};
    38	    for m in schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.target==name){needed(schema,&m.source,out);}
    39	    for k in t.columns.values(){if let C::Ref{table}=k.required(){needed(schema,table,out);}}
    40	    for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{for dep in kernel::dependencies(r){needed(schema,&dep,out);}}
    41	}
    42	fn snapshot(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    43	    snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,false)

    45	fn snapshot_mode(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize,timed:bool)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    46	    let mut names=BTreeSet::new();needed(&bound.schema,&inv.table,&mut names);
    47	    if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy{for dep in kernel::dependencies(policy){needed(&bound.schema,&dep,&mut names);}}
    48	    if let Some(mut syntax)=inv.syntax.clone(){super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut syntax,&mut |e| {
    49	        if let ast::Expr::Read(r)|ast::Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=e {needed(&bound.schema,&r.table,&mut names);}
    50	    });}
    51	    if let Some(op)=&inv.operation && let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==op){for kind in p.program.args.values(){if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind{needed(&bound.schema,table,&mut names);}}}
    52	    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();
    53	    let mut enum_case=variant;
    54	    for name in names {
    55	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&name];
    56	        if bound.schema.maintain_for(&name).is_some(){continue;}
    57	        let mut fields=BTreeMap::new();fields.insert("id".into(),id(&name));
    58	        let mut principal_index=0usize;
    59	        for (n,k) in &t.columns{
    60	            if n=="id"||t.engine_field(n)||t.maintained.contains_key(n){continue;}
    61	            let sample_variant=if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){let n=enum_case % variants.len();enum_case/=variants.len();n}else if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal){let n=((variant>>(3+principal_index.min(5)))&1)|(variant&4);principal_index+=1;n}else{variant};
    62	            let value=sample(k,sample_variant)
    63	                .ok_or_else(||format!("{name}.{n}: asset ledger/required sample is not available"))?;
    64	            fields.insert(n.clone(),value);
    65	        }
    66	        if timed || t.read_lifetime_sensitive() {for (n,k) in &t.columns {if matches!(k.required(),C::Time)&&(!k.is_optional()||variant&4!=0) {
    67	            fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_ref()==Some(n){2000}else if timed{1000}else{-1000}));
    68	        }}}
    69	        for constraint in &t.constraints{if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=constraint
    70	            && matches!(fields.get(field),Some(Value::String(_))) {fields.insert(field.clone(),Value::String("x".repeat((*min).max(1) as usize)));}}
    71	        // Named primary keys used by role probes must select the challenged
    72	        // viewer; changing the enum represents a current-role change while
    73	        // derived assignment/membership rows remain present.
    74	        if name!=inv.table {for (i,(n,k)) in t.columns.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k,C::Principal)).enumerate(){fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Id(if variant & (1 << (i+3)) == 0 {VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into())); let _=k;}}
    75	        out.insert(name,vec![Value::Row(fields.into())]);
    76	    }
    77	    Ok(out)
    78	}

    82	        let t=&self.schema.tables[table];let Some(idx)=t.index(index)else{return Ok(false)};

    86	    }

    90	fn live(t:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,row:&Value)->bool{
    91	    !t.expired(row,NOW)&&t.not_before.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|match row.field(c){Ok(Value::Int(n))=>*n<=NOW,Ok(Value::Null)=>true,_=>false})
    92	}
    93	fn policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value)->Result<bool,String>{
    94	
    95	    let Some(p)=inv.policy.as_ref() else{return inv.syntax.as_ref().and_then(|e|super::concrete::evaluate(e,row,rows,&bound.schema,viewer)).ok_or_else(||"concrete policy expression is unknown/unsupported (including clock and typed literal coercions)".into());};

   100	}
   101	fn policy_in(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,engine:&mut Engine)->Result<bool,String>{
   102	    let Some(p)=&inv.policy else{return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);};

   104	    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}

   109	fn program(name:&str,kind:ProgramKind,body:Vec<Stmt>)->Program{Program{name:name.into(),kind,args:BTreeMap::new(),body,read_time_granularity_ms:None,returned_shapes:BTreeMap::new(),native:false}}
   110	fn engine(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probe:Program)->Result<Engine,String>{

   113	fn engine_with(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
   114	    engine_on_store(bound,rows,probes,Store::memory().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)
   115	}
   116	fn engine_on_store(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>,store:Store)->Result<Engine,String>{
   117	    let mut programs=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();programs.extend(probes);
   118	    let mut engine=Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),programs,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   119	    fn hydrate(engine:&mut Engine,kind:&C,value:&Value)->Result<Value,String>{
   120	        match (kind,value) {
   121	            (C::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>hydrate(engine,inner,v),
   122	            (C::List{of,..}|C::ListRange{of,..},Value::Array(xs))=>xs.iter().map(|v|hydrate(engine,of,v)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map(Value::Array),
   123	            (C::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>engine.store_asset(VIEWER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|format!("verified image fixture: {e}")),
   124	            (C::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(engine,VIEWER,types),
   125	            _=>Ok(value.clone()),
   126	        }
   127	    }
   128	    let mut seeded=rows.iter().filter(|(name,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(name).is_none()).map(|(name,rs)|{
   129	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];
   130	        let clean=rs.iter().map(|r|if let Value::Row(fields)=r {Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect())}else{r.clone()}).collect();
   131	        (name.clone(),clean)
   132	    }).collect::<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>();
   133	    for (table,values) in &mut seeded {for row in values {for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns {
   134	        if let Ok(v)=row.field(column) {
   135	            let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,v)?;
   136	            if let Value::Row(fields)=row {fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}
   137	        }
   138	    }}}
   139	    engine.seed_at(seeded.iter().flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>(),NOW).map_err(|e|format!("generated snapshot rejected by schema: {e}"))?;
   140	    let actual=readback(&engine)?;
   141	    for (name,values) in &actual {for row in values {
   142	        snapback4_core::engine::validate::validate_row(&bound.schema,name,row).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   143	        for c in &bound.schema.tables[name].constraints {if let Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table,index,key}=c {
   144	            let rule=RuleExpr::Exists{table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),key:key.clone(),provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed,equalities:Vec::new()};
   145	            if engine.fixture_policy(&rule,snapback4_core::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL,None,row,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=rules::Verdict::Readable {return Err(format!("{name}: trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"));}
   146	        }}
   147	    }}
   148	    Ok(engine)
   149	}
   150	fn replay(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,word:&str,row:&Value)->Result<(bool,Value),String>{
   151	    let name="$security.probe";let table=inv.table.clone();let key=id(&table);
   152	    let read=Read{site:"security challenge".into(),table:table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),key:vec![Expr::Literal(key.clone())],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed};
   153	    let mut seeds=rows.clone();
   154	    let probe=match word{
   155	        "read"=>program(name,ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(read))]),
   156	        "insert"=>{seeds.remove(&table);program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Insert{table,row:Expr::Literal(row.clone())},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])},
   157	        "delete"=>program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Delete{table,key:Expr::Literal(key)},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]),
   158	        "update"=>{
   159	            let definition=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
   160	            let changed=definition.columns.iter().filter(|(n,_)|n.as_str()!="id"&&!definition.engine_field(n))
   161	                .filter(|(n,_)|inv.column.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|c==*n))
   162	                .filter(|(n,_)|!definition.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(n))))
   163	                .find_map(|(n,k)| {let old=row.field(n).ok()?;let next=match (k.required(),old){
   164	                    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
   165	                    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|v|Some(Value::String((*v).clone()))!=Some(old.clone()))?.clone()),
   166	                    (C::Text{max},Value::String(v)) if v.chars().count()<*max as usize=>Value::String(format!("{v}x")),
   167	                    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(v))=>Value::Int(v+1),_=>return None};
   168	                    Some((n.clone(),next))});
   169	            let Some((column,value))=changed else{return Err("no valid changed mutable-column sample".into());};
   170	            program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Update{table,key:Expr::Literal(key),patch:Expr::Literal(Value::Map([(column,value)].into_iter().collect()))},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])
   171	        },_=>return Err("row integrity/transition challenge is not an operation admission test".into()),
   172	    };
   173	    let mut engine=engine(bound,&seeds,probe)?;
   174	    if word=="read"{return engine.query(name,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map(|q|(q.data!=Value::Null,row.clone())).map_err(|e|e.to_string());}
   175	    let outcome=engine.mutate(name,None,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   176	    if !matches!(outcome.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..}){return Ok((false,row.clone()));}
   177	    let next=engine.store().rows(&inv.table).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.into_iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
   178	    if word=="delete"{return if next.is_none(){Ok((true,row.clone()))}else{Err("delete returned sent without removing the target".into())};}
   179	    let next=next.ok_or("sent write has no committed target image")?;
   180	    if word=="update" {
   181	        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
   182	        let changed=table.columns.keys().filter(|c|!table.engine_field(c)&&!table.maintained.contains_key(*c))
   183	            .any(|c|row.field(c).ok()!=next.field(c).ok()&&inv.column.as_ref().is_none_or(|column|column==c));
   184	        if !changed{return Err("sent no-op/implicit-only update has no changed declared target column".into());}
   185	    }
   186	    Ok((true,next))
   187	}
   188	fn readback(engine:&Engine)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{engine.schema().tables.keys().map(|t|engine.store().rows(t).map(|rs|(t.clone(),rs)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).collect()}
   189	
   190	fn operation_preflight(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<(),String>{
   191	    let Some(name)=&inv.operation else{return Ok(());};
   192	    let p=&bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==name).ok_or("operation did not bind")?.program;
   193	    if !super::census::body(&p.body){return Err(format!("{name}: opaque/loop/scan path requires write-boundary tracing"));}
   194	    let rule=inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("operation policy outside rule fragment")?;
   195	    if rule.any_term(&|t|!matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))){return Err("operation policy requires write-boundary row-image tracing".into());}
   196	    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
   197	    if writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).count()>1{return Err(format!("{name}: multiple target writes need executed-effect tracing; net images do not identify delete/reinsert"));}
   198	    let unstable=kernel::dependencies(rule).iter().any(|dep|{
   199	        let hits=writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==dep).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   200	        !hits.is_empty()&&!(dep==&inv.table&&hits.len()==1&&hits[0].1=="insert")
   201	    });
   202	    if unstable||!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty()||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.maintained.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())){
   203	        return Err("operation policy dependency may change before/after its write boundary; snapshot correspondence unproved".into());
   204	    }Ok(())
   205	}
   206	
   207	/// Schema-only engine challenges. Successful synthetic operations refute the
   208	/// declared table-authority contract, not application-operation reachability.
   209	pub fn run(bound:&Bound)->Campaign{run_selected(bound,None)}
   210	/// `check` searches unproved direct obligations; unsearched blockers remain
   211	/// explicitly unproved. Default `test` passes None and runs the whole set.
   212	pub fn run_selected(bound:&Bound,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>)->Campaign{
   213	    let mut out=Campaign::default();
   214	    // A direct component theorem needs no repeated concrete search for that
   215	    // same component. Indirect observers, temporal reads and actual authored
   216	    // operations below still run; the whole declaration remains unproved.
   217	    let discharged=super::analyze(bound).invariants.into_iter()
   218	        .filter(|v|v.direct_rule==Status::Proved&&v.invariant.operation.is_none()
   219	            && (matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"|"insert"|"delete")
   220	                || matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"update"|"write")&&bound.schema.tables[&v.invariant.table].written_by.is_empty()))

   221	        .map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   222	    let mut assertions=bound.invariants.clone();
   223	    for inv in &bound.invariants {
   224	        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue;}
   225	        if inv.operation.is_some()||matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"){continue;}
   226	        for p in &bound.programs {if p.program.kind!=ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}
   227	            let relevant=ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(table,kind)|table==&inv.table&&(inv.target=="write"||inv.target==*kind||*kind=="upsert"));
   228	            if relevant{let mut op=inv.clone();op.operation=Some(p.program.name.clone());assertions.push(op);}
   229	        }
   230	    }
   231	    for inv in &assertions{
   232	        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue;}
   233	        if sites.is_some_and(|sites|!sites.contains(&inv.site.to_string())){continue;}
   234	        if inv.operation.is_none()&&discharged.contains(&inv.site.to_string()){continue;}
   235	        if let Err(why)=operation_preflight(bound,inv){out.unsupported.insert(why);continue;}
   236	        let mut found=false;
   237	        let combinations=bound.schema.tables.values().flat_map(|t|t.columns.values()).filter_map(|k|if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){Some(variants.len())}else{None}).fold(1usize,usize::saturating_mul).max(24);
   238	        if combinations>512{out.unsupported.insert("schema enum Cartesian matrix exceeds 512 samples; remaining tuples are not covered".into());}
   239	        for sample in 0..=combinations.min(512) {
