   241	            let mut rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   242	            // Try explicit policy field literals before the unchanged enum
   243	            // matrix. A value such as status='cancelled' need not wait behind
   244	            // hundreds of unrelated enum tuples. This is only a candidate;
   245	            // ordinary validation and engine replay still decide refutation.
   246	            if sample==0 {
   247	                fn pin(rule:&RuleExpr,row:&mut Value)->bool{match rule{
   248	                    RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(v))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(v))|
   249	                    RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(v),RuleTerm::Field(f))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Literal(v),RuleTerm::Field(f))=>{
   250	                        if let Value::Row(fields)=row&&fields.contains_key(f){fields.insert(f.clone(),v.clone());true}else{false}
   251	                    },
   252	                    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().fold(false,|any,r|pin(r,row)||any),
   253	                    RuleExpr::Not(r)=>pin(r,row),_=>false,
   254	                }}
   255	                if !inv.policy.as_ref().zip(rows.get_mut(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first_mut())).is_some_and(|(p,row)|pin(p,row)){continue;}
   256	            }
   257	            let mut seeded=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(Read{site:"security read".into(),table:inv.table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),key:vec![Expr::Literal(id(&inv.table))],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed}))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
   258	            let rows=match readback(&seeded){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
   259	            let Some(row)=rows.get(&inv.table).and_then(|r|r.first())else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no generated ordinary row",inv.table));break;};
   260	            for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER,"account:security-no-role","service:security"]{
   261	                if inv.target=="always" {
   262	                    match policy(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,None,row){
   263	                        Ok(false)=>{out.attempted+=1;out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::TrustedSeedOnly,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:"$security.seed_at".into(),effect:"always".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:rows.clone(),rule_path:"trusted seed ingress; all typed and final-exists constraints rechecked".into(),scope:"actual seed_at commit/read-back; ordinary application reachability is not claimed".into()});found=true;},
   264	                        Ok(true)=>{out.attempted+=1;},Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);}
   265	                    }if found{break;}continue;
   266	                }
   267	                if let Some(operation)=&inv.operation {
   268	                    let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==operation)else{continue;};
   269	                    let permitted=policy(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,None,row);
   270	                    let Some(args)=arguments(&p.program.args,variant)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{operation}: arguments need fixture assets or unsupported samples"));continue;};
   271	                    out.attempted+=1;
   272	                    let mut seeded=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
   273	                    let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut seeded,&p.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{operation}: argument ledger unavailable: {why}"));continue;}};
   274	                    match seeded.mutate(operation,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)) {
   275	                        Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})=>{
   276	                            let current=readback(&seeded).unwrap_or_default();
   277	                            let changed=current.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|!rows.get(&inv.table).is_some_and(|old|old.contains(r))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   278	                            for next in changed {
   279	                                let old=rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|old|old.field("id").ok()==next.field("id").ok());
   280	                                // An upsert must be attributed to its executed arm;
   281	                                // a column contract cannot be refuted by editing a sibling.
   282	                                if inv.target=="update"&&old.is_none() || inv.target=="insert"&&old.is_some() || inv.target=="delete" {continue;}
   283	                                if let Some(column)=&inv.column && old.is_some_and(|old|old.field(column).ok()==next.field(column).ok()){continue;}
   284	                                if permitted==Ok(false){out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:operation.clone(),effect:if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"}.into(),row:next.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:rows.clone(),rule_path:format!("authored operation {operation}"),scope:"actual authored mutation committed a changed image from a schema-valid seeded snapshot".into()});found=true;break;}
   285	                            }
   286	                        },_=>out.refused+=1,
   287	                    }
   288	                    if found{break;}continue;
   289	                }
   290	                for word in super::effects(inv){
   291	                    let mut admission_rows=rows.clone();if word=="insert"{admission_rows.remove(&inv.table);}
   292	                    let judged=if word=="read"{policy_in(bound,inv,&admission_rows,viewer,Some(row),row,&mut seeded)}else{policy(bound,inv,&admission_rows,viewer,(word!="insert").then_some(row),row)};
   293	                    let allowed=match judged{Ok(v)=>v,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
   294	                    if allowed{continue;}out.attempted+=1;
   295	                    let replayed=if word=="read" {seeded.query("$security.seed",Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map(|q|(q.data!=Value::Null,row.clone())).map_err(|e|e.to_string())}else{replay(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,word,row)};
   296	                    match replayed{
   297	                        Ok((false,_))=>out.refused+=1,
   298	                        Ok((true,next))=>{
   299	                            let mut args=BTreeMap::new();
   300	                            if word=="update"{let changes=bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.keys().filter(|c|row.field(c).ok()!=next.field(c).ok()).filter_map(|c|next.field(c).ok().map(|v|(c.clone(),v.clone()))).collect();args.insert("$syntheticPatch".into(),Value::Map(changes));}
   301	                            out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args,viewer:viewer.into(),operation:format!("$security.{}.{}",inv.table,word),effect:word.into(),row:next,old:(word=="update"||word=="delete").then(||row.clone()),supporting_rows:admission_rows.clone(),rule_path:bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].rules.for_effect(super::effect(word)).quarry(),scope:if word=="update"{"schema-valid seeded snapshot; old-image policy false at an actual changed update; committed next image and patch shown; application reachability is not claimed".into()}else{"schema-valid seeded snapshot; synthetic engine table operation; application reachability is not claimed".into()}});found=true;break;},
   302	                        Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: {e}",inv.table,word));},
   303	                    }
   304	                }
   305	                if found{break;}
   306	            }
   307	            if found{break;}
   308	        }
   309	    }
   310	    column_reads::generated(bound,&mut out,sites);
   311	    paired::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
   312	    timed::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
   313	    out.unsupported.insert("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require a live server; engine principals are authenticated contexts".into());
   314	    out.unsupported.insert("offline/revoked-device access and retained history require device boundary qualification".into());out
   315	}
   316	pub fn apply(report:&mut Report,campaign:&Campaign){report.campaign_coverage.extend(campaign.unsupported.iter().cloned());for c in &campaign.violations{for v in &mut report.invariants{if v.invariant.site.to_string()==c.invariant_site{
   317	    if v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|old.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace)&&c.reachability!=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace{continue;}
   318	    v.status=Status::Refuted;v.counterexample=Some(c.clone());v.reasons.push("concrete policy-false snapshot admitted by the actual engine; finite test evidence, not a reachability theorem".into());
   319	}}}}
   320	
   321	fn arguments(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,variant:usize)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
   322	    use ir::ArgType as A;
   323	    fn one(k:&A,v:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match k{
   324	        A::Ref{table}=>id(table),A::Principal=>Value::Id(if v&1==0{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),A::Id=>Value::Id("security-argument".into()),A::Cursor|A::Optional(_)=>Value::Null,
   325	        A::Text{min,max,..}=>Value::String("z".repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize)),A::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(v%variants.len())?.clone()),A::Int|A::Decimal{..}|A::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),A::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),A::Time=>Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000),A::Bool=>Value::Bool(v&2==0),A::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),A::Json{..}=>Value::Null,A::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),A::Image{..}=>Value::String("$security:image".into()),A::File{..}=>Value::String("$security:file".into()),_=>return None,
   326	    })}
   327	    args.iter().map(|(n,k)|one(k,variant).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
   328	}
   329	
   330	fn materialize_arguments(engine:&mut Engine,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,values:&BTreeMap<String,Value>,viewer:&str)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Value>,String>{
   331	    fn one(e:&mut Engine,k:&ir::ArgType,v:&Value,viewer:&str)->Result<Value,String>{Ok(match (k,v){
   332	        (ir::ArgType::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>one(e,inner,v,viewer)?,
   333	        (ir::ArgType::List{of:Some(inner),..}|ir::ArgType::ListRange{of:Some(inner),..},Value::Array(xs))=>Value::Array(xs.iter().map(|v|one(e,inner,v,viewer)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
   334	        (ir::ArgType::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>e.store_asset(viewer,&PNG,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.column_value(),
   335	        (ir::ArgType::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(e,viewer,types)?,
   336	        _=>v.clone(),
   337	    })}
   338	    values.iter().map(|(n,v)|one(engine,&args[n],v,viewer).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
   339	}
   340	
   341	/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
   342	/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
   343	/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
   344	pub fn fixture_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
   345	    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
   346	    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
   347	}
   348	pub fn operations(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
   349	    fixture_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
   350	    assets::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
   351	    let fixtures=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   352	    let has_fixture=fixtures.values().any(|r|!r.is_empty());
   353	    if fixtures.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{campaign.unsupported.insert("operation fixture matrix exceeds 1000 rows; provide a focused security fixture".into());return;}
   354	    let mut viewers=histories::viewers(bound);
   355	    for (table,rows) in &fixtures{for row in rows{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!viewers.contains(p){viewers.push(p.clone());}}}}
   356	    let quota=(8192/bound.programs.len().max(1)).clamp(16,512);
   357	    for p in &bound.programs{
   358	        if !matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.program.native{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: trusted/system/opaque operation",p.program.name));continue;}
   359	        let target=bound.schema.visible_tables().find(|(t,_)|super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t)).or_else(||bound.schema.visible_tables().next()).map(|(t,_)|t.clone());
   360	        let Some(target)=target else{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no table population for generated arguments",p.program.name));continue;};
   361	        let dummy=Invariant{table:target,target:"read".into(),column:None,predicate:"allow".into(),site:crate::Site::default(),policy:Some(RuleExpr::Allow),operation:Some(p.program.name.clone()),requirements:Vec::new(),syntax:None,unsupported:None};
   362	        let cases=histories::fixture_cases(&p.program.args,&fixtures,&viewers,campaign);
   363	        let mut exercised=0;
   364	        'cases:for (variant,mut args) in cases.into_iter().enumerate(){
   365	            let rows=if has_fixture{fixtures.clone()}else{match snapshot(bound,&dummy,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));break;}}};
   366	            if !has_fixture{for (arg,kind) in &p.program.args {if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind&&let Some(row)=rows.get(table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())&&let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(arg.clone(),id.clone());}}}
   367	            for viewer in &viewers{
   368	                if exercised==quota{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: operation matrix capped at {quota} calls to share the 8192-call budget across every operation; remaining arguments/viewers not covered",p.program.name));break 'cases;}
   369	                exercised+=1;
   370	                let mut engine=match if has_fixture {fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.matrix",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))}{Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));continue;}};
   371	                let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: argument ledger unavailable: {why}",p.program.name));continue;}};
   372	                campaign.attempted+=1;*campaign.operations.entry(p.program.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   373	                if p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query{
   374	                    match engine.query(&p.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(_)=>{},Err(e) if e.code=="E_INPUT"=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated arguments rejected as E_INPUT",p.program.name));},Err(_)=>campaign.refused+=1,}
   375	                }else{
   376	                    match engine.mutate(&p.program.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>campaign.refused+=1,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: matrix engine error {e}",p.program.name));},_=>{},}
   377	                }
   378	            }
   379	        }
   380	    }
   381	}
   382	
   383	fn fork(_bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
   384	    if !probes.is_empty(){return Err("fixture probes must be authored operations".into());}
   385	    fixture.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())
   386	}
//! A campaign fork retains activated semantics without running adoption again.
use super::*;
impl Engine {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — isolation includes entropy, assets and ephemeral ownership.
    pub fn fork_fixture(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        if self.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.ephemeral.is_some()) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated ephemeral-hub snapshot".into()));
        }
        if self.presence.is_some(){return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated external presence snapshot".into()));}
        let store=self.store.fork_memory()?;
        let identity=store.meta("store_id")?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture store has no identity".into()))?;
        Ok(Self {
            timeline_schedule:timeline::Schedule::default(),
            ephemeral:std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(identity,self.generation,&self.schema)),
            store,schema:self.schema.clone(),programs:self.programs.clone(),ceilings:self.ceilings,
            entropy:crate::ids::seed(),minter:crate::ids::Mint::default(),generation:self.generation,
            expiry_after:self.expiry_after.clone(),dev_effects:self.dev_effects,
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native:self.native.clone(),
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native_logs:native::Logs::default(),presence:None,
        })
    }
}

impl Engine {
    /// Judge independent fixture intent with the engine's authorized rule hops.
    pub fn fixture_policy(&mut self,rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,next:&Value,now:i64)->Result<rules::Verdict,RuntimeError>{
        let tx=self.store.read_transaction()?;
        let context=if viewer==crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL{Context::system()}else{Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new())};
        let mut runner=Runner::new(&tx,&self.schema,context.at(now),Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(),Cut::Abort),0);
        let bytes=runner.rule_scratch(rule);
        runner.authorized_scratch(bytes,&[],|runner,allowance|{
            let mut probe=read::RuleProbe{runner,allowance};
            rules::evaluate_verdict(rule,viewer,old,next,false,&mut probe)
        })
    }
}
//! Isolated complete fixtures, including captured authority and asset ownership.
use super::*;
impl Store {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — fork committed state, never reinterpret stored rows as new seed input.
    pub fn fork_memory(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        let mut fork = Self::memory()?;
        {
            let backup = rusqlite::backup::Backup::new(&self.conn, &mut fork.conn)?;
            backup.run_to_completion(256, std::time::Duration::ZERO, None)?;
        }
        fork.catalog = self.catalog.clone();
        fork.identity = self.identity.clone();
        fork.server = self.server;
        if self.assets_dir.exists() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&fork.assets_dir).map_err(io)?;
            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&self.assets_dir).map_err(io)? {
                let entry = entry.map_err(io)?;
                if !entry.file_type().map_err(io)?.is_file() {
                    return Err(RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset ledger contains a non-file".into()));
                }
                std::fs::copy(entry.path(), fork.assets_dir.join(entry.file_name())).map_err(io)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(fork)
    }
}

fn io(e:std::io::Error)->RuntimeError {RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string())}

impl Store {
    /// Independent valid worlds share verified bytes, never source row state.
    pub fn empty_with_asset_ledger(&self)->Result<Self,RuntimeError>{
        let fresh=Self::memory()?;
        let ids=self.conn.prepare("SELECT id FROM _assets")?.query_map([],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
        for id in ids{
            let mut asset=self.asset(&id)?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset disappeared".into()))?;
            asset.home=None;asset.released_at=None;
            fresh.record_asset(&asset)?;
        }
        if self.assets_dir.exists(){
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&fresh.assets_dir).map_err(io)?;
            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&self.assets_dir).map_err(io)?{
                let entry=entry.map_err(io)?;
                if !entry.file_type().map_err(io)?.is_file(){return Err(RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset is not a regular file".into()));}
                std::fs::copy(entry.path(),fresh.assets_dir.join(entry.file_name())).map_err(io)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(fresh)
    }
}
//! CLI security reporting; compile/test evidence share one analyzer.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#5-exposure-report — full audit is opt-in, intent notes are on ordinary check.
use super::*;
use snapback4_lang::security::{self,Report};
mod http;
pub(super) fn unauthenticated(bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    match http::run(bound,sources){Ok(_)=>true,Err(why)=>{eprintln!("security HTTP failure: {why}");false}}
}
pub(super) fn report(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,options:&Options)->Report{
    let mut report=security::analyze(bound);
    if let Ok(sources)=project.sources(){security::add_source_notes(&mut report,&sources,bound);}
    let mut campaign=security::challenges::Campaign::default();
    if !bound.invariants.is_empty(){
        let mut selection=options.clone();
        if !selection.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&project.root.join("fixtures").is_dir(){selection.flags.insert("fixture".into(),project.root.join("fixtures").to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
        if selection.flags.contains_key("fixture"){
            match super::test_fixture::engine_at(project,bound,&selection,1_800_000_000_000){
                Ok(fixture)=>security::challenges::fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,&mut campaign),
                Err(why)=>for verdict in &mut report.invariants{verdict.reasons.push(format!("fixture reachability not covered: {why}"));},
            }
        }else{for verdict in &mut report.invariants{verdict.reasons.push("no trusted fixture: application reachability not covered; seed/synthetic witnesses are non-failing".into());}}
    }
    let sites=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved&&!campaign.violations.iter().any(|c|c.invariant_site==v.invariant.site.to_string()&&c.reachability==security::challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace)).map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect();
    let synthetic=security::challenges::run_selected(bound,Some(&sites));
    security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&synthetic);
    security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&campaign);report
}
pub fn audit(options:&Options)->i32{
    let project=project_or_exit();
    let bound=compiled_or_exit(match project.compile(){Ok(b)=>b,Err(e)=>{eprintln!("audit: {e}");return 2;}},options.json);
    let report=report(&project,&bound,options);
    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::to_string(&report).unwrap());}
    else {print!("{}",report.text_with_all(options.flags.contains_key("all")));}
    if report.refuted(){1}else{0}
}
pub(super) fn summary(report:&Report,all:bool){
    for v in &report.invariants {if !all&&v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability!=security::challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace){eprintln!("security: {} refuted by seed/synthetic witness only (non-failing; --all for detail)",v.invariant.target_label());continue;}eprintln!("security {:?}: {} {}",v.status,v.invariant.target_label(),v.invariant.predicate);for why in &v.reasons{eprintln!("  {why}");}if let Some(c)=&v.counterexample{eprintln!("  counterexample {}",serde_json::to_string(c).unwrap());}}
    for note in &report.notes{eprintln!("{} {}: {} — {}",note.site,note.code,note.message,note.rewrite);}
    if !report.invariants.is_empty(){eprintln!("security: audit --json lists paths, exclusions and grant writers; guide security explains proof scope");}
}

    10	fn mutable_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->bool {
    11	    let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(name)else{return false;};
    12	    let roles=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name);
    13	    if roles.is_empty(){return false;}

    15	    fn supplied(value:&serde_json::Value,roles:&BTreeSet<String>)->bool{
    16	        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Object").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
    17	        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Literal").and_then(|v|v.get("Map").or_else(||v.get("Row"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
    18	        true // Dynamic patches cannot establish that role fields are untouched.
    19	    }
    20	    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,name:&str,roles:&BTreeSet<String>,update:bool,delete:bool)->bool{
    21	        match value{
    22	            serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>{
    23	                for tag in ["Update","Upsert","WriteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
    24	                    let target=if tag=="WriteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
    25	                    if update&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name)&&body.get(if tag=="Upsert"{"row"}else{"patch"}).is_some_and(|v|supplied(v,roles)){return true;}
    26	                }}
    27	                for tag in ["Delete","DeleteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
    28	                    let target=if tag=="DeleteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
    29	                    if delete&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name){return true;}
    30	                }}
    31	                xs.values().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete))
    32	            },serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete)),_=>false,
    33	        }
    34	    }

    38	        let delete=system||!matches!(t.rules.delete,RuleExpr::Deny);
    39	        writes(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),name,&roles,update,delete)

    42	fn recipient_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str,branch:&RuleExpr,grant:Option<&str>,roster:&str,effect:&str)->bool{
    43	    if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==name&&(i.target==effect||i.target=="write"&&matches!(effect,"insert"|"update"))&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::dependencies(p).contains(roster))){return true;}

    48	            for (c,key) in equalities{if matches!(key,RuleTerm::Viewer){recipients.insert(c.clone());}}
    49	        }}

    56	pub(super) fn notes(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    57	    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    58	    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
    59	        if t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable(){note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by every signed-in user: {}",t.rules.read.quarry()),"confirm the spec permits these columns and declare read intent; snapback4 audit lists inherited grants and their writers");}
    60	        if let RuleExpr::Exists{table:roster,key,equalities,..}=&t.rules.read
    61	            && equalities.is_empty()&&key.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer)) {
    62	            note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by any signed-in user with a matching {roster} row; no particular role or row ownership is required"),"confirm that every roster member should see all these columns; declare intent from the privacy spec and inspect audit");
    63	        }
    64	        for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
    65	            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
    66	            if kind.contains_asset() && (kind.is_optional() || matches!(kind.required(),C::List{..}|C::ListRange{min:0,..})) {
    67	                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE",format!("{name}.{column} permits omitted or empty media evidence"),"if the spec requires evidence, declare a required image/file or [image] 1..N; otherwise retain optionality deliberately (guide security)");
    68	            }
    69	        }
    70	        for leaf in rule_parts(&t.rules.insert) {
    71	            if let RuleExpr::Created {table:parent,..}=leaf
    72	                && bound.schema.table(parent).is_some_and(|p| p.rules.insert.disjuncts().iter().any(|r| matches!(r,RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(_)))))
    73	                && bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other| [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r| rule_parts(r).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if table==name&&matches!(key.as_slice(),[RuleTerm::Viewer])&&equalities.is_empty())))) {
    74	                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_VACUOUS_RULE",format!("{name}: `created {parent}` can bootstrap authority trusted globally by a viewer-only probe; a caller may create their own global administrator grant"),"provision global authority only through an operator/service seat or existing authorized admin; group creation must scope every later grant to that group (guide deploy, first-admin setup)");
    75	            }
    76	        }
    77	        for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)] {
    78	          for branch in rule.disjuncts(){
    79	            if role_test(branch){continue;}
    80	            for dep in kernel::dependencies(branch){if let Some(g)=bound.schema.table(&dep) {
    81	                let rosters=[&g.rules.insert,&g.rules.update].into_iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).filter(|r|mutable_role(bound,r)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    82	                if rosters.iter().any(|r|recipient_role(bound,name,branch,Some(&dep),r,effect))&&(role_test(&g.rules.insert)||role_test(&g.rules.update)){
    83	                    note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: `{}` trusts retained {dep} grants while current role in {} can change",branch.quarry(),rosters.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),"recheck the current role in this branch or revoke every derived grant transactionally on role changes; guide security");
    84	                }
    85	            }}
    86	            if !reader_columns(branch).is_empty()&&[&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update].into_iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).any(|r|mutable_role(bound,&r)&&recipient_role(bound,name,branch,None,&r,effect))&&(role_test(&t.rules.insert)||role_test(&t.rules.update)) {
    87	                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: stored principal `{}` grants authority after a mutable role changes",branch.quarry()),"recheck the current role as well as stored attribution; guide security");
    88	            }
    89	          }
    90	        }
    91	    }
    92	    // Inspect original syntax for argument flow and distinct authored codes.
    93	    // Keeping lints outside Bound.notes preserves the compiler's existing
    94	    // diagnostic contract; check/audit expose this security advice separately.
    95	    let _=exposures;
    96	    notes
    97	}
    98	pub(super) fn source_notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    99	    let mut out=Vec::new();
   100	    let documents=sources.iter().map(|(file,source)|crate::parse_document(file,source).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   101	    let catalog=super::super::catalog::Catalog::build(documents.clone(),&mut Vec::new());
   102	    for doc in documents{
   103	        for p in &doc.programs {
   104	            let mut body=p.prelude.clone();body.extend(p.body.clone());
   105	            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
   106	            let mut guarded=BTreeSet::new();
   107	            for statement in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body) {
   108	                if let Stmt::Require{condition,..}=statement {
   109	                    let mut condition=condition.clone();
   110	                    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut condition,&mut |e|if let Expr::Exists{read,..}=e {
   111	                        for key in &read.keys {if let Expr::Name{name,..}=&key.value {guarded.insert(name.clone());}}
   112	                    });
   113	                }
   114	            }
   115	            for (write,context) in super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p){
   116	                let Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=&write else{continue;};
   117	                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{continue;};
   118	                let role_columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
   119	                for column in &role_columns {if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column)||matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert)&&t.defaults.contains_key(column))&&bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other|[&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(table))) {
   120	                    let mut targets=fields.iter().filter(|f|&f.name==column).filter_map(|f|match &f.value{Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(s),..})=>Some(s.clone()),_=>None}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   121	                    if !fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column)&&let Some(snapback4_core::Value::String(role))=t.defaults.get(column).map(|d|d.value(NOW_FOR_DEFAULTS,"$advisory")){targets.push(role);}
   122	                    let chosen=fields.iter().find(|f|&f.name==column);
   123	                    let dynamic=chosen.is_none()||chosen.is_some_and(|f|!matches!(f.value,Some(Expr::Literal{..})));
   124	                    if let Some(field)=chosen {
   125	                        let argument=field.value.as_ref().and_then(|e|if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e{Some(name.as_str())}else{None}).unwrap_or(field.name.as_str());
   126	                        if let Some(arg)=p.args.iter().find(|a|a.name==argument)&&let ast::TypeKind::Enum{variants}= &arg.type_.kind {targets=variants.clone();}
   127	                    }
   128	                    let requirements=catalog.tables.get(table).map(|info|super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context)).unwrap_or_default();
   129	                    let effective_op=if chosen.is_none()&&*op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{ast::WriteOp::Insert}else{*op};
   130	                    if let Some(risk)=super::privilege::risk(bound,table,column,&targets,effective_op,&requirements,dynamic)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),"$privilege".into())) {
   131	                        note(&mut out,site,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION",format!("{}: {risk}; review the grant's delegation policy",p.name),"restrict role assignment to authorized delegation or an operator seat; use guide staging's first-admin recipe (intentional open enrollment may permit this)");
   132	                    }
   133	                }}
   134	            }
   135	            super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e|{
   136	                let Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=e else{return};
   137	                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{return};
   138	                let readers=reader_columns(&t.rules.read);
   139	                for f in fields.iter(){
   140	                    let arg=f.value.as_ref().and_then(|e|if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e{Some(name.as_str())}else{None}).unwrap_or(f.name.as_str());
   141	                    if !guarded.contains(arg)&&readers.contains(&f.name)&&p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==arg)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),f.name.clone())){
   142	                        note(&mut out,site,"N_ARGUMENT_GRANT",format!("{} writes argument {arg} straight into reader-grant column {table}.{}",p.name,f.name),"require the chosen principal's current role and relationship before granting access; caller authority alone does not validate the recipient");
   143	                    }
   144	
   145	                }
   146	            });
   147	            // Distinct absent and denial codes disclose logical existence to
   148	            // callers admitted by a broader table rule, even without payload.
   149	            fn refusals(body:&[Stmt],bound:&Bound,absent:&mut Option<String>,codes:&mut Vec<(Site,String)>){for s in body{match s{
   150	                Stmt::Bind{value:Expr::Assert{value,code,..},..}=>{
   151	                    *absent=None;
   152	                    if let Expr::Read(read)=value.as_ref()&&let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(&read.table){
   153	                        let own=read.keys.iter().any(|k|matches!(&k.value,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"));
   154	                        if !t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable()&&!own {
   155	                            // A table read gate already conflates hidden and absent.
   156	                            // Only broader program-level privacy intent can make
   157	                            // its later refusal distinguish private existence.
   158	                            if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==read.table&&i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&t.rules.read,policy)==Ok(false))){*absent=Some(code.clone());}
   159	                        }
   160	                    }
   161	                },
   162	                Stmt::Require{site,code,..}=>if let Some(a)=absent.as_ref()&&a!=code{codes.push((site.clone(),format!("lookup absence {a}, later denial {code}")));},
   163	                Stmt::If{then_body,else_body,..}=>{refusals(then_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);refusals(else_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);},
   164	                Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>refusals(body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes),_=>{}}}}
   165	            let mut codes=Vec::new();refusals(&p.body,bound,&mut None,&mut codes);
   166	            for (site,why) in codes.into_iter().take(1){note(&mut out,&site,"N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE",format!("{}: {why}; row readership is broader than declared private-existence intent",p.name),"use the same absence/denial code and message for private existence, or tighten the row audience");}
   167	            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Job {
   168	                fn guards(body:&[Stmt],out:&mut Vec<Expr>){for s in body {match s{
   169	                    Stmt::Require{condition,..}=>out.push(condition.clone()),
   170	                    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{out.push(condition.clone());guards(then_body,out);guards(else_body,out);},
   171	                    Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>guards(body,out),_=>{}
   172	                }}}
   173	                let mut job_guards=Vec::new();guards(&p.body,&mut job_guards);
   174	                let clock=job_guards.iter().any(|g|{let mut g=g.clone();let mut found=false;super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut g,&mut |e|if matches!(e,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"){found=true;});found});
   175	                if !clock{continue;}
   176	                let mut writes=BTreeSet::new();let mut b=p.body.clone();
   177	                super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut b,&mut |e|if let Expr::Write{table,fields,..}=e&&fields.iter().any(|f|f.name=="status"){writes.insert(table.clone());});
   178	                for table in writes {for m in doc.programs.iter().filter(|m|m.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation){
   179	                    let mut aliases=BTreeSet::new();
   180	                    for s in &m.body{if let Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=s{
   181	                        let v=if let Expr::Assert{value,..}=value{value.as_ref()}else{value};
   182	                        if matches!(v,Expr::Read(r) if r.table==table){aliases.extend(names.clone());}
   183	                    }}
   184	                    let mut checks=Vec::new();guards(&m.body,&mut checks);let(mut status,mut now)=(false,false);
   185	                    for mut c in checks{super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut c,&mut |e|match e{
   186	                        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"=>now=true,
   187	                        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="status"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Name{name,..} if aliases.contains(name))=>status=true,_=>{}
   188	                    });}
   189	                    let module = std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("schema");
   190	                    let job_name = if module == "schema" { p.name.clone() } else { format!("{module}.{}", p.name) };
   191	                    let already_named = bound.notes.iter().any(|n| n.code == "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" && n.site == m.site
   192	                        && n.message.contains(&format!("column {table}.status)"))
   193	                        && n.message.contains(&format!("scheduled job {job_name} ")));
   194	                    if status&&!now&&!already_named{note(&mut out,&m.site,"N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE",format!("{} gates on {table}.status while clock job {} changes that status; writes can run between the deadline and sweep",m.name,p.name),"enforce the deadline against server now in the mutation too; a sweep cleans up state but does not enforce an exact deadline");}
   195	                }}
   196	            }
   197	        }
   198	    }
   199	    out
   200	}
     1	//! Saved-source fixture selection, business cases and the query sweep.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#9-wave-13-saved-version-proofs-day3-2026-10-16 — contemporary fixtures belong to the selected saved source.
     3	use super::*;
     4	
     5	const CASE_SUMMARY: &str = "SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES";
     6	
     7	fn case_count(path: &std::path::Path) -> Result<usize, String> {
     8	    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
     9	    Ok(text.lines().filter(|line| {
    10	        let line = line.trim();
    11	        !line.is_empty() && !line.starts_with("//")
    12	    }).count())
    13	}
    14	
    15	/// `test`: run every argument-free query as each persona against the store
    16	/// (optionally loaded from a fixture) and print the meters beside the
    17	/// ceilings, so a builder reads numbers instead of being refused on a bound.
    18	pub fn test(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    19	    let project = match proof_source::project(options, "source") { Ok(project) => project, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    20	    let sources = match proof_source::sources(&project, options, "source") { Ok(sources) => sources, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    21	    let bound = compiled_or_exit(proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "source"), options.json);
    22	    let mut defaults = options.clone();
    23	    let mut skipped_business_cases = 0;
    24	    if !defaults.flags.contains_key("fixture") {
    25	        let saved = options.flags.get("source").map(std::path::Path::new).map(|path| {
    26	            if path.is_dir() { path.join("fixtures") }
    27	            else if path.file_name().is_some_and(|name| name == "schema.q") { path.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures") }
    28	            else { path.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures", path.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy())) }
    29	        });
    30	        if let Some(fixture) = saved.filter(|path| path.is_dir()) {
    31	            eprintln!("test: using {} (saved version's contemporary fixtures; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display());
    32	            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
    33	        } else if project.root.join("fixtures").is_dir() {
    34	            let fixture = project.root.join("fixtures");
    35	            if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
    36	                defaults.flags.insert("saved-fixture-fallback".into(), String::new());
    37	                eprintln!("test: no contemporary fixtures for saved source; validating only declared-table files in {}; pass --fixture <dir> for a complete contemporary case suite", fixture.display());
    38	                let cases = fixture.join(test_cases::FILE);
    39	                if cases.is_file() {
    40	                    skipped_business_cases = match case_count(&cases) { Ok(count) => count, Err(error) => { eprintln!("test: {error}"); return 1; } };
    41	                    eprintln!("test: skipped {} — working cases may target another release; pass --fixture <dir> to run explicitly", cases.display());
    42	                }
    43	            } else { eprintln!("test: using {} (default fixture directory; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display()); }
    44	            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
    45	        } else if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
    46	            eprintln!("test: no saved or working fixtures; query sweep only; pass --fixture <dir> for business cases");
    47	        }
    48	    }
    49	    let options = &defaults;
    50	    // test applies dev's cast: a persona dev would refuse is not run as an ordinary viewer.
    51	    // Residency measures any principal in the data (scale fixtures name thousands), so it notes instead.
    52	    if let Some(why) = options.flags.get("as").and_then(|names| names.split(',').find_map(|name| test_cases::undeclared_persona(name, &bound.personas))) {
    53	        if !options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
    54	            eprintln!("test --as: {why}");
    55	            return 1;
    56	        }
    57	        eprintln!("test --residency --as: measuring an undeclared principal; dev would refuse it as a persona");
    58	    }
    59	    let _owner = if options.flags.contains_key("fixture") || options.flags.contains_key("source") { None } else {
    60	        match crate::store_owner::command(&project.store_path()) {
    61	            Ok(owner) => Some(owner),
    62	            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
    63	        }
    64	    };
    65	    let fixture_now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    66	    let opened = match &_owner {
    67	        Some(owner) => owner.open_engine(&bound, std::time::Duration::from_secs(5), fixture_now).map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
    68	        None => test_fixture::engine_at(&project, &bound, options, fixture_now),
    69	    };
    70	    let mut engine = match opened {
    71	        Ok(engine) => engine,
    72	        Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
    73	    };
    74	    let personas = residency::personas(&bound, options);
    75	    let sweep_personas = if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") { Default::default() } else {
    76	        match test_sweep::load(options.flags.get("fixture"), &bound) {
    77	            Ok(matrix) => matrix,
    78	            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
    79	        }
    80	    };
    81	    if let Some(persona) = options.flags.get("as") && !sweep_personas.is_empty() {
    82	        println!("test --as {persona} overrides test.sweep.json because --as requests one deliberate global probe; for: {}", sweep_personas.keys().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
    83	    }
    84	    if options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
    85	        let verbose = options.flags.contains_key("verbose");
    86	        if let Err(error) = residency::measure(&mut engine, &bound, &personas, verbose) { eprintln!("residency measurement failed: {error}"); return 1; }
    87	        if !verbose {
    88	            if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
    89	                println!("{CASE_SUMMARY} {{\"run\":0,\"skipped\":{skipped_business_cases}}}");
    90	            }
    91	            if !options.flags.contains_key("fixture-only") {
    92	                eprintln!("test --residency executed no business cases; use --fixture-only for acquisition measurement, or omit --residency to run the cases");
    93	                return 1;
    94	            }
    95	            return 0;
    96	        }
    97	    }
    98	    let mut security_campaign = snapback4_lang::security::challenges::run(&bound);
    99	    if options.flags.contains_key("fixture"){
   100	        match test_fixture::engine_at(&project,&bound,options,snapback4_lang::security::challenges::NOW){
   101	            Ok(security_fixture)=>snapback4_lang::security::challenges::operations(&bound,&security_fixture,&mut security_campaign),
   102	            Err(why)=>{security_campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("trusted fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
   103	        }
   104	    }else{
   105	        security_campaign.unsupported.insert("no trusted fixture files for the fixed campaign clock; development store is not replayed".into());
   106	        if let Ok(store)=snapback4_core::Store::memory(){
   107	            match snapback4_core::Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()),snapback4_lang::security::challenges::NOW){
   108	                Ok(isolated)=>snapback4_lang::security::challenges::operations(&bound,&isolated,&mut security_campaign),
   109	                Err(why)=>{security_campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("schema matrix unavailable: {why}"));}
   110	            }
   111	        }
   112	    }
   113	    let security_http_ok=security::unauthenticated(&bound,&sources);
   114	    if security_http_ok{security_campaign.unsupported.retain(|s|!s.starts_with("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require"));}
   115	    println!("security histories: {} ordered short operation sequences replayed",security_campaign.sequences);
   116	    for why in &security_campaign.boundary_failures {println!("security boundary failure: {why}");}
   117	    println!("security operation matrix: {} operations exercised; unknown authorization intent is not inferred from success",security_campaign.operations.len());
   118	    let reachable=security_campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|c.reachability==snapback4_lang::security::challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace).count();
   119	    println!("security tests: {} attempted, {} refused, {reachable} reachable violations; {} seed/synthetic observations (non-failing); finite campaign, not proof",security_campaign.attempted,security_campaign.refused,security_campaign.violations.len()-reachable);
   120	    for c in security_campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|options.flags.contains_key("all")||c.reachability==snapback4_lang::security::challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace) {println!("security violation: {}",serde_json::to_string(c).unwrap());}
   121	    for reason in &security_campaign.unsupported {println!("security not covered: {reason}");}
   122	    let (mut passed, mut failed_cases) = (0, 0);
   123	    if let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture").filter(|_| !options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback")) {
   124	        match test_cases::run_verbose(&mut engine, &bound, fixture, &sources, fixture_now, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")) {
   125	            Ok((p, f)) => { passed = p; failed_cases = f; }
   126	            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
   127	        }
   128	    }
   129	    let ceilings = engine.ceilings();
   130	    let mut failures = 0;
   131	    println!("test: {:<28} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>10} {:>10}", "operation (persona)", "examined", "entries", "probes", "steps", "heap B", "output B");
   132	    println!("      {:<28} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>10} {:>10}", "ceilings", ceilings.examined, ceilings.index_entries, ceilings.rule_probes, ceilings.steps, ceilings.heap_bytes, ceilings.output_bytes);
   133	    println!("legend: examined=rows visited; entries=index visits; probes=rule lookups; steps=interpreter work; heap B=charged heap; output B=encoded answer.");
   134	    println!("verdicts: complete=whole answer; capped=partial; refused=expected-case or program-require refusal; denied=unexpected refusal; not run=no coverage; case OK=expectation passed.");
   135	    let mut skipped = Vec::new();
   136	    let mut held_not_run = 0;
   137	    let mut near = Vec::new();
   138	    let (mut complete, mut capped, mut refused_derived, mut expected) = (0usize, 0usize, 0usize, 0usize);
   139	    let explicit = options.flags.get("fixture").filter(|_| !options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback")).map(|fixture| test_cases::explicit(fixture, &bound, fixture_now)).unwrap_or_default();
   140	    for program in &bound.programs {
   141	        if program.program.kind != ProgramKind::Query {
   142	            continue;
   143	        }
   144	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — a server fixture is not the device's held partition.
   145	        if bound.schema.operations.get(&program.program.name).is_some_and(|fact| fact.held) {
   146	            println!("      not run: {} is held (device only)", program.program.name);
   147	            held_not_run += 1;
   148	            continue;
   149	        }
   150	        let selected = if options.flags.contains_key("as") { &personas }
   151	            else { sweep_personas.get(&program.program.name).unwrap_or(&personas) };
   152	        for persona in selected {
   153	            let principal = if persona.contains(':') { persona.clone() } else { persona_principal(persona) };
   154	            let name = &program.program.name;
   155	            let cases: Vec<&test_cases::Explicit> = explicit.iter().filter(|case| case.op == *name && case.principal == principal).collect();
   156	            // Arguments: the persona's own case for this query when it has
   157	            // one (the builder's choice, run as written); otherwise derived
   158	            // from the data: a reference is a row of its table, preferring
   159	            // one whose answer is not empty (the first row is often another
   160	            // persona's, hidden), a principal is the persona, a text is the
   161	            // shortest that fits. What cannot be derived is named, never
   162	            // skipped silently.
   163	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.2 — wave 7: derived arguments tested nothing when the first row was hidden from the persona.
   164	            let mut candidates: Vec<(BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::Value>, Option<&str>)> = Vec::new();
   165	            if let Some(case) = cases.iter().find(|case| case.args.is_some()) {
   166	                let decoded: Option<BTreeMap<_, _>> = case.args.as_ref().and_then(Json::as_object).and_then(|fields| fields.iter().map(|(arg, value)| {
   167	                    let kind = program.program.args.get(arg)?;
   168	                    wire::from_json(kind, value).ok().map(|value| (arg.clone(), value))
   169	                }).collect());
   170	                if let Some(args) = decoded { candidates.push((args, Some(case.at.as_str()))); }
   171	            }
   172	            let mut missing = Vec::new();
   173	            if candidates.is_empty() {
   174	                let rows = program.program.args.values().filter_map(|kind| match kind { ir::ArgType::Ref { table } => engine.store().rows(table).ok().map(|rows| rows.len()), _ => None }).max().unwrap_or(1).clamp(1, DERIVED_ROWS);
   175	                for at in 0..rows {
   176	                    let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
   177	                    for (arg, kind) in &program.program.args {
   178	                        match sample_arg(&engine, kind, &principal, at) {
   179	                            Some(value) => { args.insert(arg.clone(), value); }
   180	                            None if at == 0 => missing.push(format!("{arg}: {}", arg_word(kind))),
   181	                            None => {}
   182	                        }
   183	                    }
   184	                    if !missing.is_empty() { break; }
   185	                    candidates.push((args, None));
   186	                }
   187	            }
   188	            if !missing.is_empty() {
   189	                skipped.push(format!("{name} ({persona}): needs {}", missing.join(", ")));
   190	                continue;
   191	            }
   192	            // The first answer with rows wins; else the first answer; else the first refusal.
   193	            let mut chosen: Option<(usize, BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::Value>, Option<&str>, Result<snapback4_core::QueryOutcome, snapback4_core::Refusal>, f64)> = None;
   194	            let tried = candidates.len();
   195	            for (args, source) in candidates {
   196	                let started = Instant::now();
   197	                let result = engine.query(name, Context::new(principal.clone(), args.clone()).at(fixture_now));
   198	                let ms = started.elapsed().as_secs_f64() * 1000.0;
   199	                let rank = match &result { Ok(outcome) if !empty_answer(&outcome.data) => 0, Ok(_) => 1, Err(_) => 2 };
   200	                if chosen.as_ref().is_none_or(|(best, ..)| rank < *best) { chosen = Some((rank, args, source, result, ms)); }
   201	                if rank == 0 || source.is_some() { break; }
   202	            }
   203	            let Some((rank, args, source, result, ms)) = chosen else { continue };
   204	            let shown = if args.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!(" {}", serde_json::to_string(&args.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), wire::to_json(v))).collect::<serde_json::Map<_, _>>()).unwrap_or_default()) };
   205	            let arguments = shown.clone();
   206	            let shown = match source { Some(at) => format!("{shown} (from {at})"), None => shown };
   207	            let derived = !args.is_empty() && source.is_none();
   208	            let label = format!("{name} ({persona})");
   209	            match result {
   210	                Ok(outcome) => {
   211	                    let m = outcome.metered;
   212	                    let state = if outcome.complete { "complete" } else { "capped" };
   213	                    if outcome.complete { complete += 1 } else { capped += 1 }
   214	                    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.22.1 — report approach to a ceiling on the fixture, separately from actual cuts.
   215	                    if outcome.capped_at.is_none() && outcome.word_cut_at.is_none() {
   216	                        if let Some(cost) = crate::serve::read_cost::near_ceiling(m, ceilings) { near.push(format!("{label} {cost}")); }
   217	                    }
   218	                    let empty = if derived && rank == 1 && tried > 1 { format!(" — empty for each of {tried} derived rows: give it yours in {}", test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() };
   219	                    println!("      {label:<28} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>10} {:>10}  {state} in {ms:.1} ms{shown}{empty}", m.examined, m.index_entries, m.rule_probes, m.steps, m.heap_bytes, m.output_bytes);
   220	                }
   221	                // A refusal the persona's own case expects is what the builder
   222	                // asked for: say so instead of suggesting a case that exists.
   223	                Err(refusal) if cases.iter().any(|case| case.expect.as_deref() == Some(refusal.code.as_str())) => {
   224	                    expected += 1;
   225	                    let at = cases.iter().find(|case| case.expect.as_deref() == Some(refusal.code.as_str())).map(|case| case.at.as_str()).unwrap_or_default();
   226	                    println!("      {label:<28} refused {} as expected ({at}){}", refusal.code, if source == Some(at) { arguments } else { shown });
   227	                }
   228	                Err(refusal) if refusal.family == "rule" => {
   229	                    // A program's own `require` refusing this persona is
   230	                    // information, not a failure: the persona is not in that
   231	                    // group. The caller chose nothing on this pass, whether
   232	                    // the arguments were derived or there are none (wave 5
   233	                    // lane 059: an argument-free gate counted as denied).
   234	                    // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.2 — the derived pass never fails on the program's own require.
   235	                    refused_derived += 1;
   236	                    if let Some(at) = source {
   237	                        println!("      {label:<28} refused {} on your arguments{shown} — the case at {at} runs below", refusal.code);
   238	                    } else if derived {
   239	                        let rows = if tried > 1 { format!(" (each of {tried} rows)") } else { String::new() };
   240	                        println!("      {label:<28} refused {} on derived arguments{shown}{rows} — give it yours in {}: {{\"op\":\"{name}\",\"as\":\"{persona}\",\"args\":{{..}}}}", refusal.code, test_cases::FILE);
   241	                    } else {
   242	                        println!("      {label:<28} refused {} by its own require (not a failure: {persona} is not admitted) — pin it in {}: {{\"op\":\"{name}\",\"as\":\"{persona}\",\"expect\":\"{}\"}}", refusal.code, test_cases::FILE, refusal.code);
   243	                    }
   244	                }
   245	                Err(refusal) => {
   246	                    failures += 1;
   247	                    println!("      {label:<28} denied {}: {}{shown}", refusal.code, refusal.message);
   248	                }
   249	            }
   250	        }
   251	    }
   252	    for line in near.iter().take(10) { println!("near ceiling: {line}"); }
   253	    if near.len() > 10 { println!("near ceiling: {} more query/persona reads passed half a ceiling without a cut", near.len() - 10); }
   254	    for line in &skipped {
   255	        println!("      not run: {line} — add a row of that table to the fixture, or a case in {}", test_cases::FILE);
   256	    }
   257	    // One line a script or an agent can read: derived refusals and unrun
   258	    // queries are not failures, but they are not coverage either.
   259	    let no_cases = passed + failed_cases == 0 && !options.flags.contains_key("fixture-only");
   260	    let security_failed = security_campaign.violations.iter().any(|c|c.reachability==snapback4_lang::security::challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace) || !security_campaign.boundary_failures.is_empty() || !security_http_ok;
   261	    let verdict = if failures > 0 || failed_cases > 0 || no_cases || security_failed { "FAIL" } else if !skipped.is_empty() || held_not_run > 0 { "incomplete" } else { "pass" };
   262	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#9 — derived probe refusals are expected exploration, not failed cases.
   263	    println!("test: {complete} complete, {capped} capped, {expected} refused as your cases expect, {refused_derived} probe refusals on derived arguments (expected), {} not run, {failures} denied{} — {verdict}",
   264	        skipped.len() + held_not_run, if passed + failed_cases > 0 { format!("; cases: {passed} passed, {failed_cases} failed") } else if options.flags.contains_key("fixture") { format!("; no {} (see snapback4 guide testing)", test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() });
   265	    // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11-wave-14-truthful-starter-custody-day4 — query-sweep reads are not application business cases.
   266	    if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
   267	        println!("{CASE_SUMMARY} {{\"run\":{},\"skipped\":{skipped_business_cases}}}", passed + failed_cases);
   268	    }
   269	    if no_cases {
   270	        eprintln!("test: no business cases executed ({skipped_business_cases} skipped); add test.cases.jsonl, or use --fixture-only when fixture loading/query probes are the intended check");
   271	    }
   272	    if failures > 0 || failed_cases > 0 || no_cases || security_failed { 1 } else { 0 }
   273	}

+fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str){

+fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value)->bool{
+    use crate::types::Ty;
+    match ty{
+        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key),
+        Ty::Optional(inner)=>typed_disclosure(inner,data,table,key),
+        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|matches!(r,Value::Row(_))&&r.field("id").ok()==Some(key))),
+        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure(inner,r,table,key))),
+        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure(ty,v,table,key))),
+        _=>false,
+    }
 }
+fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
+    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c=="id")){
+        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],None),p)==Ok(true));
+        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
+        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}
 
+        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
+            let committed=steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
+            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&v.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count()>=committed){continue;}
+            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
+            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
+            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
+            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
+            for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!p.program.native){
+                let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program.args,rows,actors,out);
+                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
+                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
+                    out.attempted+=1;
+                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&q.complete&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key){
+                        violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
+                        if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query returned a compiler-typed row of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
+                        continue 'actors;
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
+            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
+            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
+            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
+            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
+            for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!p.program.native){
+                let p=&bp.program;
+                let cases=fixture_cases(&p.args,rows,actors,out);
+                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
+                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
+                    out.attempted+=2;
+                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
+                    if a==b{continue;}
+                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){continue;}
+                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
+                    if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
+                    continue 'actors;
+                }
+            }
+        }}
+    }
+}
+fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
+    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
+}
+fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
+    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
+        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
+            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
+            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
+            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
+            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
+            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
+                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
+    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
+    if !super::super::census::body(&p.body){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: loop/opaque write boundary requires executed-effect tracing",p.name));return;}
+    for inv in &bound.invariants{
+        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always")||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
+        let judged;
+        let inv=if inv.target=="always"{inv}else{
+            let Some(stable)=stability::necessary(bound,p,inv)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: unknown/implicit policy changes need executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;};
+            judged=stable;&judged
+        };
+        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
+        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
+        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
+        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1!="delete"&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: commit-end/implicit retirement is not an executed delete",p.name));}
+        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1=="delete"{
+            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|live(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],old)&&!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
+                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
+                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
+                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
+            }
+        }
+        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
+            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()&&live(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],r));
+            if old==Some(row){continue;}
+            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
+            if inv.target!="always"&&inv.operation.is_none()&&!super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
+            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
+            if inv.target=="always" {
+                if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
+            }else{
+                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
+                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table) {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: write policy dependency changes within the operation; tracing required",p.name));continue;}
+                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row,prior)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false)){
+                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+fn role_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
+    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
+    let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
+    let Ok(mut judge)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return out;};
+    for (table,_) in ir::write_tables(p){
+        let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&table);
+        if !columns.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))){continue;}
+        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
+        for row in initial.get(&table).into_iter().flatten(){
+            let principal=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal).then(||row.field(c).ok()).flatten());
+            let Some(principal)=principal else{continue;};
+            let mut targets=Vec::new();
+            for case in cases{
+                let mut args=case.clone();
+                for (name,kind) in &p.args{match kind{
+                    ir::ArgType::Principal=>{args.insert(name.clone(),principal.clone());},
+                    ir::ArgType::Ref{table:target} if target==&table=>{if let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(name.clone(),id.clone());}},_=>{}
+                }}
+                if !targets.contains(&args){targets.push(args);}
+            }
+            let mut row_cases=Vec::new();
+            for actor in actors{
+                let owner=columns.iter().filter_map(|c|t.written_by.get(c)).any(|a|row.field(&a.owner).ok()==Some(&Value::Id(actor.clone())));
+                let gate=judge.fixture_policy(&t.update_rule_judged(),actor,Some(row),row,NOW).ok();
+                if !owner&&gate!=Some(rules::Verdict::Readable){continue;}
+                for args in &targets{row_cases.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
+            }
+            groups.push(row_cases);
+        }
+    }
+    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(case)=g.get(n){out.push(case.clone());}}}
+    out
+}

+fn mutable_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->bool {
+    let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(name)else{return false;};
+    let roles=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name);
+    if roles.is_empty(){return false;}
+    let mutable=roles.iter().any(|c|!t.constraints.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(c))));
+    fn supplied(value:&serde_json::Value,roles:&BTreeSet<String>)->bool{
+        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Object").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
+        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Literal").and_then(|v|v.get("Map").or_else(||v.get("Row"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
+        true // Dynamic patches cannot establish that role fields are untouched.
+    }
+    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,name:&str,roles:&BTreeSet<String>,update:bool,delete:bool)->bool{
+        match value{
+            serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>{
+                for tag in ["Update","Upsert","WriteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
+                    let target=if tag=="WriteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
+                    if update&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name)&&body.get(if tag=="Upsert"{"row"}else{"patch"}).is_some_and(|v|supplied(v,roles)){return true;}
+                }}
+                for tag in ["Delete","DeleteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
+                    let target=if tag=="DeleteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
+                    if delete&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name){return true;}
+                }}
+                xs.values().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete))
+            },serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete)),_=>false,
+        }
+    }
+    bound.programs.iter().any(|p|{
+        let system=p.program.kind==snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job;
+        let update=mutable&&(system||!matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny)||roles.iter().any(|c|t.written_by.contains_key(c)));
+        let delete=system||!matches!(t.rules.delete,RuleExpr::Deny);
+        writes(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),name,&roles,update,delete)
+    })

+fn recipient_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str,branch:&RuleExpr,grant:Option<&str>,roster:&str,effect:&str)->bool{
+    if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==name&&(i.target==effect||i.target=="write"&&matches!(effect,"insert"|"update"))&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::dependencies(p).contains(roster))){return true;}

+            if let Some(idx)=g.index(index){for (part,key) in idx.components.iter().zip(key){if matches!(key,RuleTerm::Viewer)&&let Some(c)=part.column(){recipients.insert(c.into());}}}
+            for (c,key) in equalities{if matches!(key,RuleTerm::Viewer){recipients.insert(c.clone());}}
+        }}
